Version](section-51-20180523.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

**[[51.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.1 .sectionLabel}** Despite subsection
51(1), the Minister may make public

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information related to service and performance
  indicators provided in accordance with regulations made under
  paragraph 50(1.01)(b); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} information referred to in paragraph 50(2)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 10, s. 13
- [2023, c. 26, s. 441](#1410601-1414061){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410601-1414061.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51.1-20180523.html "Link to previous version of section 51.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidential information --- other
persons

- **[[51.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Information that is required to be
  provided under subsection 50(1.001) to persons referred to in
  subsection 50(1.1) who are subject to the legislative authority of
  Parliament or any users, other than passengers, of the national
  transportation system is, when it is received by those persons,
  confidential and must not knowingly be disclosed or made available by
  any person without the authorization of the person who provided the
  information, except for the purposes of a prosecution of a
  contravention of section 173.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safe and secure procedures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The persons referred to in subsection 50(1.1) who are
  subject to the legislative authority of Parliament and any users,
  other than passengers, of the national transportation system must
  ensure that the procedures and physical measures taken to ensure the
  confidentiality of information provided to them under this Act,
  including the keeping of electronic data, are safe and secure.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 441](#1410601-1414068){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410601-1414068.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidentiality of information --- Agency

**[[51.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.2 .sectionLabel}** Information that is
required to be provided to the Agency in accordance with regulations
made under paragraph 50(1.01)(a) is, when it is received by the Agency,
confidential and shall not knowingly be disclosed or made available by
any person without the authorization of the person who provided the
information, except for the purpose of a prosecution of a contravention
of section 173.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 10, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of information

**[[51.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.3 .sectionLabel}** Information that is
provided to the Minister or the Agency in accordance with regulations
made under paragraph 50(1.01)(a) is only to be used by the Agency for
the purpose of determining the long-haul interswitching rate referred to
in paragraph 134(1)(a) and, despite subsection 51(4) and section 51.2,
the Agency may, for that purpose, communicate the information in an
aggregated form.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 10, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

- **[[51.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Agency receives information from class 1 rail carriers or the
  Minister that is related to service and performance indicators
  provided in accordance with regulations made under paragraph
  50(1.01)(b), the Agency shall publish the information on its Internet
  site within two days after it is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information received from Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 51(4) does not apply to the publication,
  in accordance with subsection (1), of information that is received
  from the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 10, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

- **[[51.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister is of the opinion that there exists an unusual and
  significant disruption to the effective continued operation of the
  national transportation system, the Minister may, by order, require
  any persons referred to in subsection 50(1.1) who are subject to the
  legislative authority of Parliament or any users, other than
  passengers, of the national transportation system to provide to the
  Minister information, other than [[personal
  information]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 3 of the
  [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21), that the Minister considers relevant to
  the assessment of the cause of the disruption or the mitigation or
  resolution of the disruption.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order is temporary

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under this section has effect for the
  period, not exceeding 90 days, that is specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not a statutory instrument

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The order is not a statutory instrument within the
  meaning of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 51(1), the Minister may disclose
  information that has been provided to the Minister under this section
  to any person for the purposes of the mitigation or resolution of the
  disruption.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 442](#1410603-1414081){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410603-1414081.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Industry Review]{.HTitleText3} {#h-54589 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Industry overview

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  year before the end of May, the Minister shall, using the most current
  information available, prepare and lay before both Houses of
  Parliament a report providing a brief overview of the state of
  transportation in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Industry review

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every five years, the report referred to in
  subsection (1) shall be expanded to a comprehensive review of the
  state of transportation in Canada which shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the financial performance of each mode of
    transportation and its contribution to the Canadian economy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the extent to which carriers and modes of
    transportation were provided resources, facilities and services at
    public expense;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the extent to which carriers and modes of
    transportation received compensation, indirectly and directly, for
    the resources, facilities and services that were required to be
    provided as an imposed public duty;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} the long term outlook and trends in
    transportation in Canada; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other transportation matters that the Minister
    considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 52
- 2007, c. 19, s. 11
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Review of Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-54602 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory review

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall, no later than eight years after the day this
  subsection comes into force, appoint one or more persons to carry out
  a comprehensive review of the operation of this Act and any other Act
  of Parliament for which the Minister is responsible that pertains to
  the economic regulation of a mode of transportation or to
  transportation activities under the legislative authority of
  Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objective of review

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person or persons conducting the review shall
  assess whether the legislation referred to in subsection (1) provides
  Canadians with a transportation system that is consistent with the
  national transportation policy set out in section 5 and, if necessary
  or desirable, may recommend amendments to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the national transportation policy; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the legislation referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The review shall be undertaken in consultation with
  purchasers and suppliers of transportation services and any other
  persons whom the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on review

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person appointed to carry out the review has,
  for the purposes of the review, the powers of a commissioner under
  Part I of the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11) and may engage the
  services of experts, professionals and other staff deemed necessary
  for making the review at the rates of remuneration that the Treasury
  Board approves.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The review shall be completed and a report of the
  review submitted to the Minister within 18 months after the
  appointment referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling of report

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall have a copy of the report laid
  before each House of Parliament on any of the first thirty days on
  which that House is sitting after the Minister receives it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 53
- 2007, c. 19, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-53-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 53"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Review of Mergers and Acquisitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54620 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

- **[[53.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is required to
  notify the Commissioner of Competition under subsection 114(1) of the
  [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34) of a proposed transaction that
  involves a transportation undertaking shall, at the same time as the
  Commissioner is notified and, in any event, not later than the date by
  which the person is required to notify the Commissioner,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} give notice of the proposed transaction to the
    Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a proposed transaction that involves
    an air transportation undertaking, also give notice of the
    transaction to the Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice given to the Minister or to the Agency
  shall, subject to the regulations, contain the information required
  under subsection 114(1) of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34). The
  notice shall also contain any information with respect to the public
  interest as it relates to national transportation that is required
  under any guidelines that shall be issued and published by the
  Minister. After receipt of a notice, the Minister may require the
  person who has given the notice to provide further information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The guidelines referred to in subsection (2) shall
  be elaborated in consultation with the Competition Bureau and shall
  include factors that may be considered to determine whether a proposed
  transaction raises issues with respect to the public interest as it
  relates to national transportation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not statutory instruments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The guidelines referred to in subsection (2) are not
  statutory instruments within the meaning of the [Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No public interest issues

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is of the opinion that the proposed
  transaction does not raise issues with respect to the public interest
  as it relates to national transportation, the Minister shall, within
  42 days after a person gives notice under subsection (1), give notice
  of the opinion to that person, in which case sections 53.2 and 53.3 do
  not apply in respect of that transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public interest issues

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is of the opinion that the proposed
  transaction raises issues with respect to the public interest as it
  relates to national transportation, the Minister may direct the Agency
  to examine those issues under section 49 or appoint and direct any
  person to examine those issues under section 7.1 of the [Department of
  Transport Act](/eng/acts/T-18).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Agency or person, as the case may be, shall
  report to the Minister within 150 days after being directed under
  subsection (5), or within any longer period that the Minister may
  allow.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

- **[[53.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall complete a proposed transaction referred to in subsection
  53.1(1) unless the transaction is approved by the Governor in Council
  and, in the case of a transaction that involves an air transportation
  undertaking, the Agency determines that the transaction would result
  in an undertaking that is Canadian as defined in subsection 55(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner's report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner of Competition shall within 150 days
  after the Commissioner is notified of the proposed transaction under
  subsection 114(1) of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34), or within
  any longer period that the Minister may allow, report to the Minister
  and the parties to the transaction on any concerns regarding potential
  prevention or lessening of competition that may occur as a result of
  the transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to be made public

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The report shall be made public immediately after its
  receipt by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concerns relating to public interest
  and competition

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} After receipt of the Commissioner's report and any
  report given under subsection 53.1(6), but before the Minister makes a
  recommendation for the purposes of subsection (7), the Minister shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consult with the Commissioner regarding any overlap
    between any concerns that the Minister has in respect of the
    proposed transaction with regard to the public interest as it
    relates to national transportation and any concerns in respect of
    the transaction that are raised in the Commissioner's report; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} request the parties to the transaction to address

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with the Minister any concerns that the Minister
      has in respect of the transaction with regard to the public
      interest as it relates to national transportation, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the Commissioner any concerns that the
      Commissioner has regarding potential prevention or lessening of
      competition that may occur as a result of the transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measures to address concerns

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The parties to the transaction shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after conferring with the Minister regarding
    concerns referred to in subparagraph (4)(b)(i), inform the Minister
    of any measures they are prepared to undertake to address those
    concerns; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after conferring with the Commissioner regarding
    concerns identified under subparagraph (4)(b)(ii), inform the
    Commissioner of any measures they are prepared to undertake to
    address those concerns.

  The parties may propose revisions to the transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preconditions to recommendation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Before making a recommendation for the purposes of
  subsection (7), the Minister shall obtain the Commissioner's
  assessment of the adequacy of any undertaking proposed by the parties
  to address the concerns that have been identified under subparagraph
  (4)(b)(ii) and the effects of any proposed revisions to the
  transaction on those concerns.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of Governor in Council

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the Governor in Council is satisfied that it is in
  the public interest to approve the proposed transaction, taking into
  account any revisions to it proposed by the parties and any measures
  they are prepared to undertake, the Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, approve the transaction and specify
  any terms and conditions that the Governor in Council considers
  appropriate. The Governor in Council shall indicate those terms and
  conditions that relate to potential prevention or lessening of
  competition and those that relate to the public interest as it relates
  to national transportation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation of terms and conditions

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} On application by a person who is subject to terms
  and conditions specified under subsection (7), the Governor in Council
  may, on the recommendation of the Minister, vary or rescind the terms
  and conditions. If the terms and conditions to be varied or rescinded
  affect competition, the Minister shall consult with the Commissioner
  before making the recommendation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner's representations

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister directs the Agency under section 49
  to inquire into any matter or thing to assist the Minister in making a
  recommendation under subsection (7) or (8), the Agency shall give
  notice of the inquiry to the Commissioner and allow the Commissioner
  to make representations to the Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with terms and conditions

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is subject to terms and conditions
  shall comply with them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian

**[[53.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.3 .sectionLabel}** The Agency shall
determine whether a proposed transaction referred to in subsection
53.1(1) that involves an air transportation undertaking would result in
an undertaking that is Canadian as defined in subsection 55(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of divestiture --- application by
Minister

- **[[53.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a person contravenes subsection 53.2(1) or (10) with respect to a term
  or condition that relates to the public interest as it relates to
  national transportation, a superior court may, on application by the
  Minister, order the person to cease the contravention or do any thing
  that is required to be done, and may make any other order that it
  considers appropriate, including an order requiring the divestiture of
  assets. The Minister shall notify the Commissioner of Competition
  before making an application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of divestiture --- application by
  Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person contravenes subsection 53.2(10) with
  respect to a term or condition that relates to potential prevention or
  lessening of competition, a superior court may, on application by the
  Commissioner, order the person to cease the contravention or do any
  thing that is required to be done, and may make any other order that
  it considers appropriate, including an order requiring the divestiture
  of assets. The Commissioner shall notify the Minister before making an
  application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[53.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.5 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying information required in a notice under
  subsection 53.1(1); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} exempting classes of transactions from the
  application of sections 53.1 to 53.3.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence --- subsection 53.1(1)

- **[[53.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes
  subsection 53.1(1) is guilty of an offence and is liable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine not
    exceeding \$50,000; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine not exceeding
    \$25,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence --- subsection 53.2(1) or (10)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes subsection 53.2(1)
  or (10) is guilty of an indictable offence and is liable to
  imprisonment for a term not exceeding five years or to a fine not
  exceeding \$10,000,000, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an offence under subsection 53.2(10) is committed
  or continued on more than one day, the person who commits it is liable
  to be convicted for a separate offence for each day on which it is
  committed or continued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of corporations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a corporation commits an offence under
  subsection (1) or (2), any officer, director or agent or mandatary of
  the corporation who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in
  or participated in the commission of the offence is a party to and
  guilty of the offence and is liable on conviction to the punishment
  provided for the offence whether or not the corporation has been
  prosecuted or convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sections 174 and 175 do not apply

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Sections 174 and 175 do not apply in respect of an
  offence committed under subsection (1) or (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 13
:::

### [Review of Arrangements Involving Two or More Transportation Undertakings Providing Air Services]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1122281 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[53.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.7 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in sections 53.71 to 53.84.

[[arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement or
    arrangement, other than a transaction referred to in subsection
    53.1(1), involving two or more transportation undertakings providing
    air services, as defined in subsection 55(1), to, from or within
    Canada, to coordinate on any aspect of the operation or marketing of
    such services, including prices, routes, schedules, capacity or
    ancillary services and to share costs or revenues or other resources
    or benefits. ([entente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who proposes to enter
    into or has entered into an arrangement for which a notice has been
    given under subsection 53.71(1). ([partie]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122287){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122287.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

- **[[53.71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.71
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who proposes to enter
  into an arrangement may notify the Minister of that arrangement. If
  the person so notifies the Minister, they shall at the same time
  provide a copy of the notice to the Commissioner of Competition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice given under subsection (1) shall contain any
  information that is required under the guidelines that are issued and
  published by the Minister, including information that relates to
  considerations respecting competition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The guidelines referred to in subsection (2) shall be
  developed in consultation with the Competition Bureau and shall
  include factors that may be considered by the Minister to determine
  whether a proposed arrangement raises significant considerations with
  respect to the public interest under subsection (6) and, if
  applicable, to render a final decision regarding the arrangement under
  subsection 53.73(8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not statutory instruments

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The guidelines referred to in subsection (2) are not
  statutory instruments within the meaning of the [Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or the Commissioner may, after receiving
  a notice or copy of a notice under subsection (1), require any party
  to provide further information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's response

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall, within 45 days after the day on
  which he or she receives the notice with the information referred to
  in subsection (2), inform the parties and the Commissioner as to
  whether, in his or her opinion, the proposed arrangement raises
  significant considerations with respect to the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No significant public interest
  considerations

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is of the opinion that the proposed
  arrangement does not raise significant considerations with respect to
  the public interest, sections 53.72 to 53.79 do not apply to that
  arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Significant public interest
  considerations

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is of the opinion that the proposed
  arrangement raises significant considerations with respect to the
  public interest, the arrangement is subject to the review process set
  out in section 53.73.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122306){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122306.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

**[[53.72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.72 .sectionLabel}** If a notice has
been given under subsection 53.71(1), the proposed arrangement shall not
be completed without the Minister's authorization under subsection
53.73(8).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122310){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122310.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review process

- **[[53.73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.73
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister, or a person designated
  by the Minister, shall examine the proposed arrangement, if it is
  subject to the review process.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner's report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner of Competition shall, within 120
  days after the day on which he or she receives a copy of the notice
  under subsection 53.71(1) with the information referred to in
  subsection 53.71(2), report to the Minister and the parties on any
  concerns regarding potential prevention or lessening of competition
  that may occur as a result of the proposed arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless a notice is withdrawn or is deemed to have
  been withdrawn under section 53.8, the Commissioner shall make public
  a summary of the conclusions of the report that does not include any
  confidential information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communicating concerns

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall, within 150 days after the day on
  which he or she receives the notice with the information referred to
  in subsection 53.71(2), report to the parties on any concerns with
  respect to the public interest that may occur as a result of the
  proposed arrangement and provide a copy of the report to the
  Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measures to address concerns

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The parties shall, within 30 days after the day on
  which they receive the report under subsection (4), respond in writing
  to the Minister, addressing any concerns with respect to the public
  interest and competition raised by the Minister and the Commissioner
  and informing the Minister, among other things, of any measures they
  are prepared to undertake to address those concerns. The parties may
  propose amendments to the arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preliminary decision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall, after consulting with the
  Commissioner and within 45 days after the day on which he or she
  receives the response from the parties under subsection (5), render a
  preliminary decision regarding the proposed arrangement and specify
  any terms and conditions relating to the public interest and
  competition under which an authorization could be given under
  subsection (8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Response to preliminary decision

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The parties shall, within 30 days after the day on
  which they receive the Minister's preliminary decision, provide a
  response in writing to the Minister and the response may include
  proposed amendments to the terms and conditions specified in the
  decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final decision

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall, within 30 days after the day on
  which he or she receives a response from the parties under subsection
  (7), render a final decision and make public a summary of that
  decision that does not include any confidential information. The
  Minister may, if satisfied that the proposed arrangement is in the
  public interest, authorize it and specify any terms and conditions
  relating to the public interest and competition that the Minister
  considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122329){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122329.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian

**[[53.74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.74 .sectionLabel}** An authorization
given by the Minister under subsection 53.73(8) does not affect any
requirement that a transportation undertaking providing air services, as
defined in subsection 55(1), be [[Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
defined in that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122333){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122333.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Varying or rescinding terms and
conditions

**[[53.75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.75 .sectionLabel}** On application by
any party who is subject to terms and conditions of an authorization,
the Minister may, after consulting with the Commissioner of Competition,
vary or rescind the terms and conditions and shall make public a summary
of that decision that does not include any confidential information.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122337){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122337.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposed amendment to arrangement

- **[[53.76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.76
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The parties to an authorized
  arrangement may propose an amendment to that arrangement to the
  Minister and he or she may, after consulting with the Commissioner of
  Competition and considering the significance of the amendment,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorize the amendment, subject to any terms and
    conditions that the Minister may impose; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} require the parties to submit a new notice under
    subsection 53.71(1) for review of the proposed amended arrangement,
    unless the parties decide not to proceed with the amendment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No confidential information made public

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister authorizes the amendment under
  paragraph (1)(a), he or she shall make public a summary of that
  decision that does not include any confidential information.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122346){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122346.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concerns regarding authorized arrangement

- **[[53.77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.77
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, at any time after
  the second anniversary of the day on which an arrangement is
  authorized, notify the parties of any concerns raised by the
  arrangement with respect to the public interest and competition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measures to address concerns

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The parties shall, within 45 days after the day on
  which they receive the notice under subsection (1), provide a response
  in writing to the Minister, specifying, among other things, any
  measures they are prepared to undertake to address those concerns. The
  parties may propose amendments to the arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing the authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, after consulting with the Commissioner, the
  Minister determines that the arrangement is still in the public
  interest, the authorization is continued subject to any new or amended
  terms and conditions specified by the Minister to address the concerns
  referred to in subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122355){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122355.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to comply with terms and
conditions

**[[53.78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.78 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
subject to terms and conditions under subsection 53.73(8), section
53.75, paragraph 53.76(a) or subsection 53.77(3) shall comply with them.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122359){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122359.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revoking authorization --- false or
misleading information

- **[[53.79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.79
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke an
  authorization at any time if it was granted on the basis of
  information that is false or misleading in a material respect or if
  the parties fail to comply with any of the authorization's terms or
  conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revoking authorization --- other
  grounds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may also revoke the authorization of an
  arrangement if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that arrangement is significantly amended without
    prior authorization; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister, after considering any response of the
    parties to the concerns raised under subsection 53.77(1), is no
    longer satisfied that the arrangement is in the public interest.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122368){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122368.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawing notice

- **[[53.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice given under subsection 53.71(1) may be withdrawn at any time
  before a final decision is rendered under subsection 53.73(8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed withdrawal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice is deemed to have been withdrawn if the
  parties fail to respond to the Minister within any period specified in
  subsection 53.73(5) or (7) or any extended period, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of withdrawal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a notice given under subsection 53.71(1) is
  withdrawn or is deemed to have been withdrawn, section 53.72 no longer
  applies to the proposed arrangement.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122377){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122377.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

**[[53.81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.81 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may,
at the request of the parties or on the Minister's own initiative,
extend any period specified in section 53.71, 53.73 or 53.77 if the
Minister considers it appropriate in the circumstances, including when
the arrangement is exceptionally complex.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122381){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122381.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

**[[53.82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.82 .sectionLabel}** If a person
contravenes sections 53.72 or 53.78, a superior court may, on
application by the Minister, order the person to cease the contravention
or do any thing that is required to be done, and may make any other
order that it considers appropriate, including an order requiring the
divestiture of assets. The Minister shall notify the Commissioner of
Competition before making an application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122385){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122385.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence --- section 53.72 or 53.78

- **[[53.83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.83
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes section
  53.72 or 53.78 is guilty of an indictable offence and is liable to
  imprisonment for a term of not more than five years or to a fine of
  not more than \$10,000,000, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an offence under subsection (1) for the
  contravention of section 53.78 is committed or continued on more than
  one day, the person who commits it is liable to be convicted for a
  separate offence for each day on which it is committed or continued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of corporations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a corporation commits an offence under subsection
  (1), any officer, director or agent or mandatary of the corporation
  who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or participated
  in the commission of the offence is a party to and guilty of the
  offence and is liable on conviction to the punishment provided for the
  offence, whether or not the corporation has been prosecuted or
  convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sections 174 and 175 do not apply

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Sections 174 and 175 do not apply in respect of an
  offence committed under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122396){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122396.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cost recovery

- **[[53.84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.84
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make
  regulations respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fees to be paid by the parties to an
    arrangement for any activities undertaken by the Minister under
    sections 53.71 to 53.76 that are related to the arrangement,
    including the method of calculating the fees; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the refund of all or part of any fee referred to in
    paragraph (a), including the method of calculating the refund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts not to exceed cost

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fees referred to in paragraph (1)(a) shall not
  exceed the costs related to the activities undertaken by the Minister
  under sections 53.71 to 53.76 that are related to the arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remittance of fees and charges

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The fees paid in accordance with regulations made
  under subsection (1) shall be deposited to the credit of the Receiver
  General in the time and manner prescribed under those regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spending authority

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may spend the amounts deposited under
  subsection (3) in the fiscal year in which they are paid or in the
  next fiscal year.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 10, s. 14](#1037795-1122409){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037795-1122409.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54702 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of receiver not to bar
jurisdiction

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  fact that a receiver, manager or other official of a carrier, or a
  receiver of the property of a carrier, has been appointed by a court
  in Canada, or is managing or operating a mode of transportation under
  the authority of any such court, is not a bar to the exercise of any
  jurisdiction granted under this Act, but every such receiver, manager
  or official is bound to manage and operate the mode of transportation
  in accordance with this Act and with the orders, regulations and
  directions made or issued under this Act, notwithstanding the fact
  that the receiver, manager, official or person has been appointed by
  or acts under the authority of a court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation orders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Wherever by reason of insolvency, sale under mortgage
  or any other cause, a transportation undertaking or a portion of a
  transportation undertaking is operated, managed or held otherwise than
  by the carrier, the Agency or the Minister may make any order it
  considers proper for adapting and applying the provisions of this Act.

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Air Transportation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-54708 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54709 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Part,

  [[aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 3(1) of the [Aeronautics
      Act](/eng/acts/A-2); ([aéronef]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[air service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[air service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a service, provided by
      means of an aircraft, that is publicly available for the
      transportation of passengers or goods, or both; ([service
      aérien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[basic fare]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[basic fare]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fare in the tariff of the holder of a
        domestic licence that has no restrictions and represents the
        lowest amount to be paid for one-way air transportation of an
        adult with reasonable baggage between two points in Canada, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the licensee has more than one such fare
        between two points in Canada and the amount of any of those
        fares is dependent on the time of day or day of the week of
        travel, or both, the highest of those fares; ([prix de
        base]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen or a [[permanent
        resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1)
        of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
        Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5),

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a government in Canada or an agent or mandatary
        of such a government, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a corporation or entity that is incorporated or
        formed under the laws of Canada or a province, that is
        controlled in fact by Canadians and of which at least 51% of the
        voting interests are owned and controlled by Canadians and where

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} no more than 25% of the voting interests are
          owned directly or indirectly by any single non-Canadian,
          either individually or in affiliation with another person, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} no more than 25% of the voting interests are
          owned directly or indirectly by one or more non-Canadians
          authorized to provide an air service in any jurisdiction,
          either individually or in affiliation with another
          person; ([Canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian aviation document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian aviation document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 3(1) of the [Aeronautics
      Act](/eng/acts/A-2); ([document d'aviation
      canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[domestic licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence issued
      under section 61; ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[domestic service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an air service
      between points in Canada, from and to the same point in Canada or
      between Canada and a point outside Canada that is not in the
      territory of another country; ([service
      intérieur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an air service
      between Canada and a point in the territory of another
      country; ([service international]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the holder of a licence
      issued by the Agency under this Part; ([licencié]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[non-scheduled international licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-scheduled international licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      a licence issued under subsection 73(1); ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[non-scheduled international service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-scheduled international service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      an international service other than a scheduled international
      service; ([service international à la demande]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by regulations
      made under section 86; ([règlement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[scheduled international licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[scheduled international licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      licence issued under subsection 69(1); ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[scheduled international service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[scheduled international service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      international service that is a scheduled service pursuant to

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an agreement or arrangement for the provision
        of that service to which Canada is a party, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a determination made under section
        70; ([service international régulier]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[tariff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tariff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a schedule of fares, rates,
      charges and terms and conditions of carriage applicable to the
      provision of an air service and other incidental
      services. ([tarif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one corporation is affiliated with another
    corporation if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one of them is a subsidiary of the other,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} both are subsidiaries of the same corporation,
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} both are controlled by the same person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if two corporations are affiliated with the same
    corporation at the same time, they are deemed to be affiliated with
    each other;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a partnership or sole proprietorship is affiliated
    with another partnership or sole proprietorship if both are
    controlled by the same person;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is affiliated with a partnership or a
    sole proprietorship if both are controlled by the same person;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is a subsidiary of another
    corporation if it is controlled by that other corporation or by a
    subsidiary of that other corporation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is controlled by a person other than
    Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} securities of the corporation to which are
      attached more than 50% of the votes that may be cast to elect
      directors of the corporation are held, directly or indirectly,
      whether through one or more subsidiaries or otherwise, otherwise
      than by way of security only, by or for the benefit of that
      person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the votes attached to those securities are
      sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of the directors of
      the corporation;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is controlled by Her Majesty in right
    of Canada or a province if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the corporation is controlled by Her Majesty in
      the manner described in paragraph (f), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation without share
      capital, a majority of the directors of the corporation, other
      than [ex officio]{.otherLang lang="la"} directors, are appointed
      by

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the Governor in Council or the Lieutenant
        Governor in Council of the province, as the case may be, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a Minister of the government of Canada or the
        province, as the case may be; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a partnership is controlled by a person if the
    person holds an interest in the partnership that entitles the person
    to receive more than 50% of the profits of the partnership or more
    than 50% of its assets on dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (2), [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  includes an individual, a partnership, an association, a corporation,
  a trustee, an executor, a liquidator of a succession, an administrator
  or a legal representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control in fact

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, nothing in subsection (2)
  shall be construed to affect the meaning of the expression "controlled
  in fact" in the definition [[Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 55
- 2000, c. 15, s. 1
- 2001, c. 27, s. 222
- 2018, c. 10, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-55-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 55"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Part

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part does not apply to a person that uses an aircraft on behalf of the
  Canadian Armed Forces or any other armed forces cooperating with the
  Canadian Armed Forces.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specialty service exclusion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply to the operation of
  specialty services provided by aircraft, including firefighting,
  flight training, sightseeing, spraying, surveying, mapping,
  photography, parachute jumping, glider towing, helicopter-lift for
  logging and construction, airborne agricultural, industrial and
  inspection services or any other prescribed service provided by
  aircraft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergency service exclusion

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply to the provision of an air
  service if the federal government or a provincial or a municipal
  government declares an emergency under federal or provincial law, and
  that government directly or indirectly requests that the air service
  be provided to respond to the emergency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public interest

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, prohibit the provision of
  an air service under subsection (3) or require the discontinuance of
  that air service if, in the opinion of the Minister, it is in the
  public interest to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not a statutory instrument

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The order is not a statutory instrument within the
  meaning of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 56
- 2007, c. 19, s. 14
- 2018, c. 10, s. 16
:::

Version](section-56-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}

### [Prohibitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54782 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition re operation

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** No person shall operate
an air service unless, in respect of that service, the person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} holds a licence issued under this Part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} holds a Canadian aviation document; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} has the prescribed liability insurance coverage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence not transferable

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** A licence issued under
this Part for the operation of an air service is not transferable.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition re sale

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** No person shall sell,
cause to be sold or publicly offer for sale in Canada an air service
unless, if required under this Part, a person holds a licence issued
under this Part in respect of that service and that licence is not
suspended.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 59
- 2007, c. 19, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of aircraft with flight crew

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall provide all or part of an aircraft, with a flight crew,
  to a licensee for the purpose of providing an air service pursuant to
  the licensee's licence and no licensee shall provide an air service
  using all or part of an aircraft, with a flight crew, provided by
  another person except

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with regulations made by the Agency
    respecting disclosure of the identity of the operator of the
    aircraft and other related matters; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where prescribed, with the approval of the Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions and Ministerial directions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Approval by the Agency under subsection (1) is
  subject to any directions to the Agency issued by the Minister and to
  any terms and conditions that the Agency may specify in the approval,
  including terms and conditions respecting routes to be followed,
  points or areas to be served, size and type of aircraft to be
  operated, schedules, places of call, tariffs, fares, rates and
  charges, insurance, carriage of passengers and, subject to the [Canada
  Post Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-10), carriage of goods.

### [Licence for Domestic Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54801 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of licence

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** On application to the
Agency and on payment of the specified fee, the Agency shall issue a
licence to operate a domestic service to the applicant if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant establishes in the application to the
  satisfaction of the Agency that the applicant

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a Canadian,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} holds a Canadian aviation document in respect of
    the service to be provided under the licence,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has the prescribed liability insurance coverage
    in respect of the service to be provided under the licence, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} meets prescribed financial requirements; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Agency is satisfied that the applicant has not
  contravened section 59 in respect of a domestic service within the
  preceding twelve months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualification exemption

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the Minister considers it necessary or advisable in the public
  interest that a domestic licence be issued to a person who is not a
  Canadian, the Minister may, by order, on such terms and conditions as
  may be specified in the order, exempt the person from the application
  of subparagraph 61(a)(i) for the duration of the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The order is not a regulation for the purposes of the
  [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, as soon as feasible, make the name
  of the person who is exempted and the exemption's duration accessible
  to the public through the Internet or by any other means that the
  Minister considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 62
- 2013, c. 31, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-62-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 62"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory suspension or cancellation

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency shall suspend or cancel the domestic licence of a person where
  the Agency determines that, in respect of the service for which the
  licence was issued, the person ceases to meet any of the requirements
  of subparagraphs 61(a)(i) to (iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discretionary suspension or
  cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may suspend or cancel a domestic licence

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the Agency determines that, in respect of the
    service for which the domestic licence was issued, the licensee has
    contravened, or does not meet the requirements of, any regulation or
    order made under this Part or any provision of this Part other than
    subparagraphs 61(a)(i) to (iii); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 64, in accordance with a request
    from the licensee for the suspension or cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement condition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall not reinstate a domestic licence
  that has been suspended for sixty days or longer unless the licensee
  establishes to the satisfaction of the Agency that the person meets
  the prescribed financial requirements.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of discontinuance or reduction of
certain services

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a licensee proposes to discontinue a domestic service or to reduce the
  frequency of such a service to a point to less than one flight per
  week and, as a result of the proposed discontinuance or reduction,
  there will be only one licensee or no licensee offering at least one
  flight per week to that point, the licensee shall give notice of the
  proposal in prescribed form and manner to such persons as are
  prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of discontinuance of certain
  services

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If a licensee proposes to discontinue its
  year-round non-stop scheduled air service between two points in Canada
  and that discontinuance would result in a reduction, as compared to
  the week before the proposal is to take effect, of at least 50% of the
  weekly passenger-carrying capacity of all licensees operating
  year-round non-stop scheduled air services between those two points,
  the licensee shall give notice of the proposal in the prescribed form
  and manner to the prescribed persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discussion with elected officials

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A licensee shall, as soon as practicable, provide
  an opportunity for elected officials of the municipal or local
  government of the community of the point or points, as the case may
  be, to meet and discuss with the licensee the impact of the proposed
  discontinuance or reduction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licensee shall not implement a proposal referred to
  in subsection (1) or (1.1) until the expiry of 120 days, or 30 days if
  the service referred to in that subsection has been in operation for
  less than one year, after the notice is given or until the expiry of
  any shorter period that the Agency may, on application by the
  licensee, specify by order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Considerations re whether exemption to
  be granted

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In considering whether to specify a shorter period
  under subsection (2), the Agency shall have regard to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the adequacy of alternative modes of public
    transportation available at or in the vicinity of the point referred
    to in subsection (1) or between the points referred to in subsection
    (1.1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} other means by which air service to the point or
    between the points is or is likely to be provided;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether the licensee has complied with subsection
    (1.2); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the particular circumstances of the licensee.

- Definition of [[non-stop scheduled air service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[non-stop scheduled air
  service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an air service operated between
  two points without any stops in accordance with a published timetable
  or on a regular basis.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 64
- 2000, c. 15, s. 3
- 2007, c. 19, s. 17
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-64-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 64"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaints re non-compliance

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** Where, on complaint in
writing to the Agency by any person, the Agency finds that a licensee
has failed to comply with section 64 and that it is practicable in the
circumstances for the licensee to comply with an order under this
section, the Agency may, by order, direct the licensee to reinstate the
service referred to in that section

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for such a period, not exceeding 120 days after the
  date of the finding by the Agency, as the Agency deems appropriate;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} at such a frequency as the Agency may specify.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 65
- 2007, c. 19, s. 18
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 65"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unreasonable fares or rates

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, on
  complaint in writing to the Agency by any person, the Agency finds
  that a licensee, including affiliated licensees, is the only person
  providing a domestic service between two points and that a fare, cargo
  rate or increase in a fare or cargo rate published or offered in
  respect of the service is unreasonable, the Agency may, by order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disallow the fare, rate or increase;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct the licensee to amend its tariff by reducing
    the fare, rate or increase by the amounts and for the periods that
    the Agency considers reasonable in the circumstances; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct the licensee, if practicable, to refund
    amounts specified by the Agency, with interest calculated in the
    prescribed manner, to persons determined by the Agency to have been
    overcharged by the licensee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaint of inadequate range of fares
  or rates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, on complaint in writing to the Agency by any
  person, the Agency finds that a licensee, including affiliated
  licensees, is the only person providing a domestic service between two
  points and that it is offering an inadequate range of fares or cargo
  rates in respect of that service, the Agency may, by order, direct the
  licensee, for a period that the Agency considers reasonable in the
  circumstances, to publish and apply in respect of that service one or
  more additional fares or cargo rates that the Agency considers
  reasonable in the circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relevant information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When making a finding under subsection (1) or (2)
  that a fare, cargo rate or increase in a fare or cargo rate published
  or offered in respect of a domestic service between two points is
  unreasonable or that a licensee is offering an inadequate range of
  fares or cargo rates in respect of a domestic service between two
  points, the Agency may take into consideration any information or
  factor that it considers relevant, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} historical data respecting fares or cargo rates
    applicable to domestic services between those two points;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fares or cargo rates applicable to similar domestic
    services offered by the licensee and one or more other licensees,
    including terms and conditions related to the fares or cargo rates,
    the number of seats available at those fares and the cargo capacity
    and cargo container types available at those rates;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} the competition from other modes of
    transportation, if the finding is in respect of a cargo rate, an
    increase in a cargo rate or a range of cargo rates; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other information provided by the licensee,
    including information that the licensee is required to provide under
    section 83.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative domestic services

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may find that a licensee is the only
  person providing a domestic service between two points if every
  alternative domestic service between those points is, in the Agency's
  opinion, unreasonable, taking into consideration the number of stops,
  the number of seats offered, the frequency of service, the flight
  connections and the total travel time and, more specifically, in the
  case of cargo, the cargo capacity and cargo container types available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative service

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall not make an order under
  subsection (1) or (2) in respect of a licensee found by the Agency to
  be the only person providing a domestic service between two points if,
  in the Agency's opinion, there exists another domestic service that is
  not between the two points but is a reasonable alternative taking into
  consideration the convenience of access to the service, the number of
  stops, the number of seats offered, the frequency of service, the
  flight connections and the total travel time and, more specifically,
  in the case of cargo, the cargo capacity and cargo container types
  available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration of representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Before making a direction under paragraph (1)(b) or
  subsection (2), the Agency shall consider any representations that the
  licensee has made with respect to what is reasonable in the
  circumstances.

- [(6) and (7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 19, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidentiality of information

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may take any measures or make any order
  that it considers necessary to protect the confidentiality of any of
  the following information that it is considering in the course of any
  proceedings under this section:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information that constitutes a trade secret;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information the disclosure of which would likely
    cause material financial loss to, or prejudice to the competitive
    position of, the person providing the information or on whose behalf
    it is provided; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information the disclosure of which would likely
    interfere with contractual or other negotiations being conducted by
    the person providing the information or on whose behalf it is
    provided.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 66
- 2000, c. 15, s. 4
- 2007, c. 19, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 66"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tariffs to be made public

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holder of a domestic licence shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} display in a prominent place at the business
    offices of the licensee a sign indicating that the tariffs for the
    domestic service offered by the licensee, including the terms and
    conditions of carriage, are available for public inspection at the
    business offices of the licensee, and allow the public to make such
    inspections;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} publish the terms and conditions of carriage on
    any Internet site used by the licensee for selling the domestic
    service offered by the licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in its tariffs, specifically identify the basic
    fare between all points for which a domestic service is offered by
    the licensee; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} retain a record of its tariffs, and publish them on
    its Internet site, for a period of not less than three years after
    the tariffs have ceased to have effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed tariff information to be
  included

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A tariff referred to in subsection (1) shall include
  such information as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No fares, etc., unless set out in
  tariff

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a domestic licence shall not apply any
  fare, rate, charge or term or condition of carriage applicable to the
  domestic service it offers unless the fare, rate, charge, term or
  condition is set out in a tariff that has been published or displayed
  under subsection (1) and is in effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of tariff on payment of fee

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a domestic licence shall provide a copy
  or excerpt of its tariffs to any person on request and on payment of a
  fee not exceeding the cost of making the copy or excerpt.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 67
- 2000, c. 15, s. 5
- 2007, c. 19, s. 20
- [2023, c. 26, s. 453](#1410643-1414113){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}
:::

:::

26, s. 454\]]{.Repealed}

[Previous
Version](section-67.1-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 67.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unreasonable or unduly discriminatory
terms or conditions

- **[[67.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Agency finds that the holder of a domestic licence has applied
  terms or conditions of carriage applicable to the domestic service it
  offers that are unreasonable or unduly discriminatory, the Agency may
  suspend or disallow those terms or conditions and substitute other
  terms or conditions in their place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on advertising

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a domestic licence shall not advertise
  or apply any term or condition of carriage that is suspended or has
  been disallowed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 15, s. 6
- 2007, c. 19, s. 22(F)
- [2023, c. 26, s. 455](#1410646-1420825){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410646-1420825.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-67.2-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 67.2"){.PITLink}
:::

26, s. 456\]]{.Repealed}

[Previous
Version](section-67.3-20180523.html "Link to previous version of section 67.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applying decision to other passengers

**[[67.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67.4 .sectionLabel}** The Agency may, to
the extent that it considers it appropriate, make applicable to some or
to all passengers of the same flight as the complainant all or part of
its decision respecting a complaint related to any term or condition of
carriage concerning any obligation prescribed by regulations made under
paragraph 86.11(1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 10, s. 17
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of fares, etc.

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 66 to 67.2, 85.04 and 85.07
  do not apply in respect of fares, rates or charges applicable to a
  domestic service provided for under a contract between a holder of a
  domestic licence and another person whereby the parties to the
  contract agree to keep its provisions confidential.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of terms and conditions

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 66 to 67.2, 85.04 and 85.07 do not apply
  in respect of terms and conditions of carriage applicable to a
  domestic service provided for under a contract referred to in
  subsection (1) to which an employer is a party and that relates to
  travel by its employees.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions regarding exclusive use of
  services

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The parties to the contract shall not include in it
  provisions with respect to the exclusive use by the other person of a
  domestic service operated by the holder of the domestic licence
  between two points in accordance with a published timetable or on a
  regular basis, unless the contract is for all or a significant portion
  of the capacity of a flight or a series of flights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of contract required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a domestic licence who is a party to
  the contract shall retain a copy of it for a period of not less than
  three years after it has ceased to have effect and, on request made
  within that period, shall provide a copy of it to the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 68
- 2000, c. 15, s. 7
- 2007, c. 19, s. 23
- [2023, c. 26, s. 458](#1410650-1420830){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410650-1420830.html"
  role="button"}
:::
