### [Licence for Scheduled International Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54929 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of licence

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application to the Agency and on payment of the specified fee, the
  Agency shall issue a licence to operate a scheduled international
  service to the applicant if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant establishes in the application to the
    satisfaction of the Agency that the applicant

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is, pursuant to subsection (2) or (3), eligible
      to hold the licence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} holds a Canadian aviation document in respect of
      the service to be provided under the licence,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has the prescribed liability insurance coverage
      in respect of the service to be provided under the licence, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} where the applicant is a Canadian, meets the
      prescribed financial requirements; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Agency is satisfied that the applicant has not
    contravened section 59 in respect of the service to be provided
    under the licence within the preceding twelve months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility of Canadians

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, in writing, designate any Canadian
  as eligible to hold a scheduled international licence. That Canadian
  remains eligible while the designation remains in force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility of non-Canadians

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A non-Canadian is eligible to hold a scheduled
  international licence if the non-Canadian

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been designated by a foreign government or an
    agent of a foreign government to operate an air service under the
    terms of an agreement or arrangement between that government and the
    Government of Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} holds, in respect of the air service, a document
    issued by a foreign government or agent that, in respect of the
    service to be provided under the document, is equivalent to a
    scheduled international licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 69
- 2013, c. 31, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-69-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 69"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of scheduled international
service

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, in
writing to the Agency,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} determine that an international service is a
  scheduled international service; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw a determination made under paragraph (a).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions of scheduled
international licence

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any directions issued to
  the Agency under section 76, the Agency may, on the issuance of a
  scheduled international licence or from time to time thereafter, make
  the licence subject, in addition to any terms and conditions
  prescribed in respect of the licence, to such terms and conditions as
  the Agency deems to be consistent with the agreement, convention or
  arrangement pursuant to which the licence is being issued, including
  terms and conditions respecting routes to be followed, points or areas
  to be served, size and type of aircraft to be operated, schedules,
  places of call, tariffs, fares, rates and charges, insurance, carriage
  of passengers and, subject to the [Canada Post Corporation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-10), carriage of goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with terms and conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a scheduled international licence shall
  comply with every term and condition to which the licence is subject.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory suspension or cancellation

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency shall suspend or cancel a scheduled international licence where
  the Agency determines that, in respect of the service for which the
  licence was issued, the licensee ceases to meet any of the
  requirements of subparagraphs 69(1)(a)(i) to (iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discretionary suspension or
  cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may suspend or cancel a scheduled
  international licence

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the Agency determines that, in respect of the
    service for which the licence was issued, the licensee has
    contravened, or does not meet the requirements of, any regulation or
    order made under this Part or any provision of this Part other than
    subparagraphs 69(1)(a)(i) to (iii); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a request from the licensee for
    the suspension or cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement condition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall not reinstate the scheduled
  international licence of a Canadian that has been suspended for sixty
  days or longer unless the Canadian establishes to the satisfaction of
  the Agency that the Canadian meets the prescribed financial
  requirements.

### [Licence for Non-scheduled International Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54965 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of licence

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any directions issued to
  the Agency under section 76, on application to the Agency and on
  payment of the specified fee, the Agency shall issue a licence to
  operate a non-scheduled international service to the applicant if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant establishes in the application to the
    satisfaction of the Agency that the applicant

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a Canadian,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} holds a Canadian aviation document in respect of
      the service to be provided under the licence,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has the prescribed liability insurance coverage
      in respect of the service to be provided under the licence, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} meets prescribed financial requirements; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Agency is satisfied that the applicant has not
    contravened section 59 in respect of the service to be provided
    under the licence within the preceding twelve months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-Canadian applicant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any directions issued to the Agency under
  section 76, on application to the Agency and on payment of the
  specified fee, the Agency may issue a non-scheduled international
  licence to a non-Canadian applicant if the applicant establishes in
  the application to the satisfaction of the Agency that the applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} holds a document issued by the government of the
    applicant's state or an agent of that government that, in respect of
    the service to be provided under the document, is equivalent to the
    non-scheduled international licence for which the application is
    being made; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of subparagraphs (1)(a)(ii)
    and (iii) and paragraph (1)(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions of non-scheduled
international licence

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any directions issued to
  the Agency under section 76, the Agency may, on the issuance of a
  non-scheduled international licence or from time to time thereafter,
  make the licence subject, in addition to any terms and conditions
  prescribed in respect of the licence, to such terms and conditions as
  the Agency deems appropriate, including terms and conditions
  respecting points or areas to be served, size and type of aircraft to
  be operated, schedules, places of call, tariffs, fares, rates and
  charges, insurance, carriage of passengers and, subject to the [Canada
  Post Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-10), carriage of goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with terms and conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a non-scheduled international licence
  shall comply with every term and condition to which the licence is
  subject.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory suspension or cancellation

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency shall suspend or cancel a non-scheduled international licence
  where the Agency determines that, in respect of the service for which
  the licence was issued, the licensee ceases to meet any of the
  requirements of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a Canadian licensee, subparagraphs
    73(1)(a)(i) to (iii); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a non-Canadian licensee,
    subparagraphs 73(1)(a)(ii) and (iii) and paragraph 73(2)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discretionary suspension or
  cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may suspend or cancel a non-scheduled
  international licence

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the Agency determines that, in respect of the
    service for which the licence was issued, the licensee has
    contravened, or does not meet the requirements of, any regulation or
    order made under this Part or any provision of this Part other than
    the provisions referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a request from the licensee for
    the suspension or cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement condition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall not reinstate the non-scheduled
  international licence of a Canadian that has been suspended for sixty
  days or longer unless the Canadian establishes to the satisfaction of
  the Agency that the Canadian meets the prescribed financial
  requirements.

### [Issuance of International Charter Permits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-54995 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance, amendment and cancellation of
permits

**[[75.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.1 .sectionLabel}** The issuance of a
permit for the operation of an international charter to a licensee and
the amendment or cancellation of the permit shall be made in accordance
with regulations made under paragraph 86(1)(e).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 24
:::

### [Ministerial Directions for International Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-55000 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may issue directions

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the Minister determines that it is necessary or advisable to provide
  direction to the Agency in respect of the exercise of any of its
  powers or the performance of any of its duties or functions under this
  Part relating to international service,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the interest of the safety or security of
    international civil aviation,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in connection with the implementation or
    administration of an international agreement, convention or
    arrangement respecting civil aviation to which Canada is a party,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the interest of international comity or
    reciprocity,

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of enforcing Canada's rights under
    an international agreement, convention or arrangement respecting
    civil aviation or responding to acts, policies or practices by a
    contracting party to any such agreement, convention or arrangement,
    or by an agency or citizen of such a party, that adversely affect or
    lead either directly or indirectly to adverse effects on Canadian
    international civil aviation services, or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in connection with any other matter concerning
    international civil aviation as it affects the public interest,

  the Minister may, subject to subsection (3), issue to the Agency
  directions that, notwithstanding any other provision of this Part, are
  binding on, and shall be complied with by, the Agency in the exercise
  of its powers or the performance of its duties or functions under this
  Part relating to international service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nature of directions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Directions issued under subsection (1) may relate to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} persons or classes of persons to whom licences to
    operate an international service shall or shall not be issued;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the terms and conditions of such licences, or their
    variation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the suspension or cancellation of such licences;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other matter concerning international service
    that is not governed by or under the [Aeronautics
    Act](/eng/acts/A-2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concurrence required for certain
  directions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A direction by the Minister relating to a matter
  referred to in paragraph (1)(c), (d) or (e) may be issued only with
  the concurrence of the Minister of Foreign Affairs.

### [Duties and Powers of Agency]{.HTitleText2} {#h-55018 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties and functions of Agency under
international agreements, etc.

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** Where the Agency is
identified as the aeronautical authority for Canada under an
international agreement, convention or arrangement respecting civil
aviation to which Canada is a party, or is directed by the Minister to
perform any duty or function of the Minister pursuant to any such
agreement, convention or arrangement, the Agency shall act as the
aeronautical authority for Canada or perform the duty or function in
accordance with the agreement, convention, arrangement or direction, as
the case may be.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agency powers qualified by certain
agreements, etc.

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any directions issued to
  the Agency under section 76, the powers conferred on the Agency by
  this Part shall be exercised in accordance with any international
  agreement, convention or arrangement relating to civil aviation to
  which Canada is a party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variations from agreements, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1) and subject to any
  directions issued to the Agency under section 76, the Agency may issue
  a licence or suspend a licence, or vary the terms and conditions of a
  licence, on a temporary basis for international air services that are
  not permitted in an agreement, convention or arrangement relating to
  civil aviation to which Canada is a party.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agency may refuse licence --- individuals

- **[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the Agency has suspended or cancelled the licence of an individual
  under this Part or where an individual has contravened section 59, the
  Agency may, for a period not exceeding twelve months after the date of
  the suspension, cancellation or contravention, refuse to issue a
  licence in respect of an air service to the individual or to any
  corporation of which the individual is a principal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agency may refuse licence ---
  corporations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Agency has suspended or cancelled the
  licence of a corporation under this Part or where a corporation has
  contravened section 59, the Agency may, for a period not exceeding
  twelve months after the date of the suspension, cancellation or
  contravention, refuse to issue a licence in respect of an air service
  to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the corporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any person who, as a principal of the corporation,
    directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or participated in
    a contravention that gave rise to the suspension or cancellation;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any body corporate of which the corporation or the
    person referred to in paragraph (b) is a principal.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency may, by order, on such terms and conditions as it deems
  appropriate, exempt a person from the application of any of the
  provisions of this Part or of a regulation or order made under this
  Part where the Agency is of the opinion that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has substantially complied with the
    provision;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an action taken by the person is as effective as
    actual compliance with the provision; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} compliance with the provision by the person is
    unnecessary, undesirable or impractical.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption not to provide certain relief

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No exemption shall be granted under subsection (1)
  that has the effect of relieving a person from any provision of this
  Part that requires a person to be a Canadian and to have a Canadian
  aviation document and prescribed liability insurance coverage in
  respect of an air service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption not to provide certain relief
  --- section 69

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No exemption shall be granted under subsection (1)
  that has the effect of relieving a person from the provisions of
  section 69 that require, in order to be eligible to hold a scheduled
  international licence,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian to be designated by the Minister to hold
    such a licence; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a non-Canadian to be designated by a foreign
    government or an agent of a foreign government to operate an air
    service under the terms of an agreement or arrangement between that
    government and the Government of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 80
- 2013, c. 31, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry into licensing matters

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
ensuring compliance with this Part, the Agency may inquire into any
matter for which a licence, permit or other document is required under
this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licensee to provide notification

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** Every licensee shall
notify the Agency without delay, in writing, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the liability insurance coverage in respect of the
  air service for which the licence is issued is cancelled or is altered
  in a manner that results in the failure by the licensee to have the
  prescribed liability insurance coverage for that service;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensee's operations change in a manner that
  results in the failure by the licensee to have the prescribed
  liability insurance coverage for that service; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any change occurs that affects, or is likely to
  affect, the licensee's status as a Canadian.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information required

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** A licensee shall, at the
request of the Agency, provide the Agency with information or documents
available to the licensee that relate to any complaint under review or
any investigation being conducted by the Agency under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of agent required

- **[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licensee who has an agent in Canada shall, in writing, provide the
  Agency with the agent's name and address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment and notice of agent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licensee who does not have a place of business or
  an agent in Canada shall appoint an agent who has a place of business
  in Canada and, in writing, provide the Agency with the agent's name
  and address.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change of address

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** Where the address of a
licensee's principal place of business in Canada or the name or address
of the licensee's agent in Canada is changed, the licensee shall notify
the Agency in writing of the change without delay.

### [Carrier's Obligation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-55064 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Process for claims

- **[[85.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A carrier shall establish a process
  for dealing with claims related to a fare, rate, charge or term or
  condition of carriage applicable to the air service it offers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period to communicate decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The process shall include an obligation for the
  carrier, on receipt of a written request to deal with a claim, to
  communicate to the claimant its decision on the claim within 30 days
  after the day on which it received the request.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420838){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420838.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Air Travel Complaints]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1420839 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaint resolution officers

- **[[85.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Chairperson, or a person
  designated by the Chairperson, shall designate, from among the members
  and staff of the Agency, persons to act as complaint resolution
  officers for the purpose of sections 85.04 to 85.12.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limits on powers and duties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A member of the Agency or its staff who acts as a
  complaint resolution officer has the powers, duties and functions of a
  complaint resolution officer and not of the Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification --- proceedings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Proceedings before a complaint resolution officer are
  not proceedings before the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420848){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420848.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain provisions

**[[85.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.03 .sectionLabel}** Sections 17, 25 and
36.1 do not apply in respect of any matter that may be dealt with under
sections 85.04 to 85.12.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420852){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420852.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaints related to tariffs

- **[[85.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person may file a complaint in
  writing with the Agency if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person alleges that a carrier failed to apply a
    fare, rate, charge or term or condition of carriage applicable to
    the air service it offers that is set out in its tariffs;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is adversely affected by the failure to
    apply that fare, rate, charge or term or condition of carriage;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person seeks compensation or a refund as set
    out in the carrier's tariffs or compensation for expenses incurred
    as a result of that failure; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person made a written request to the carrier to
    resolve the matters to which the complaint relates but they were not
    resolved within 30 days after the day on which the request was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to deal with complaint

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A complaint resolution officer may refuse to deal
  with a complaint or, at any time, cease dealing with it if they are of
  the opinion that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the criteria set out in subsection (1) have not
    been met;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is clear on the face of the complaint that the
    carrier has complied with the obligations set out in its tariffs; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the complaint is vexatious or made in bad faith.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420866){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420866.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mediation

- **[[85.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the complaint resolution officer
  does not refuse under subsection 85.04(2) to deal with a complaint,
  they shall mediate the complaint and start the mediation no later than
  the 30th day after the day on which the complaint is filed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of mediation agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An agreement that is reached as a result of mediation
  may be filed with the Agency and, after filing, is enforceable as if
  it were an order of the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420873){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420873.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision on complaint

- **[[85.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.06
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If no agreement is reached as a
  result of mediation, and the complaint resolution officer does not
  cease dealing with the complaint under subsection 85.04(2), the
  complaint resolution officer shall, no later than the 60th day after
  the day on which the mediation started, and based on the information
  provided by the complainant and the carrier,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make an order under subsection 85.07(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make an order dismissing the complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order referred to in subsection (1) is not an
  order or decision of the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420882){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420882.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order related to tariffs

- **[[85.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.07
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the complaint resolution officer
  finds that the carrier that is the subject of the complaint has failed
  to apply a fare, rate, charge or term or condition of carriage
  applicable to the air service it offers that is set out in its
  tariffs, the complaint resolution officer may order the carrier to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply a fare, rate, charge or term or condition of
    carriage that is set out in its tariffs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} compensate the complainant for any expenses they
    incurred as a result of the carrier's failure to apply a fare, rate,
    charge or term or condition of carriage that is set out in its
    tariffs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Onus

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a complaint raises an issue as to whether a flight
  delay, flight cancellation or denial of boarding is within a carrier's
  control, is within a carrier's control but is required for safety
  reasons or is outside a carrier's control, it is presumed to be within
  the carrier's control and not required for safety reasons unless the
  carrier proves the contrary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of order and enforcement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) may be filed with
  the Agency and, after filing, is enforceable as if it were an order of
  the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420893){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420893.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior decisions to be taken into account

**[[85.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.08 .sectionLabel}** In regards to the
issue of whether a flight delay, flight cancellation or denial of
boarding is within a carrier's control, is within a carrier's control
but is required for safety reasons or is outside a carrier's control, a
complaint resolution officer who is dealing with a complaint in respect
of a flight shall take into account any prior decision on that issue
that is contained in an order made by a complaint resolution officer in
respect of that flight.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420897){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420897.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidentiality of information

- **[[85.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.09
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All matters related to the process
  of dealing with a complaint shall be kept confidential, unless the
  complainant and the carrier otherwise agree, and information provided
  by the complainant or the carrier to the complaint resolution officer
  for the purpose of the complaint resolution officer dealing with the
  complaint shall not be used for any other purpose without the consent
  of the one who provided it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply so as to prohibit

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the communication of information to the Agency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the communication of information to complaint
    resolution officers for the purpose of assisting them in the
    exercise of their powers or the performance of their duties and
    functions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the making public by the Agency of information
    under sections 85.14 and 85.15.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420907){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420907.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

**[[85.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.1 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
procedure set out in the guidelines referred to in section 85.12, a
complaint resolution officer shall deal with complaints in the manner
that they consider appropriate in the circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 15, s. 7.1
- 2007, c. 19, s. 25
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420910){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420910.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-85.1-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 85.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance by Agency

**[[85.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.11 .sectionLabel}** The Agency may, at
a complaint resolution officer's request, provide administrative,
technical and legal assistance to the complaint resolution officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420914){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420914.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines

- **[[85.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may issue guidelines

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner of and procedures for dealing
    with complaints filed under subsection 85.04(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} setting out the extent to which and the manner in
    which, in the Agency's opinion, any provision of the regulations
    applies with regard to complaints.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines binding

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A guideline is, until it is revoked or modified,
  binding on any complaint resolution officer dealing with a complaint
  filed under subsection 85.04(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each guideline shall be published on the Agency's
  website, in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in any
  other manner that the Agency considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to the guideline.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420927){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420927.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral to panel

- **[[85.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If no agreement is reached as a
  result of the mediation of a complaint under section 85.05, the
  Chairperson or a person designated by them may, at the request of the
  complaint resolution officer who conducted the mediation, and if the
  Chairperson or person designated by them, as the case may be,
  considers that the complexity of the complaint requires it, refer the
  complaint to a panel of at least two members. Those members, none of
  whom is to be the complaint resolution officer who conducted the
  mediation, shall act as the complaint resolution officers in respect
  of the complaint for the purposes of sections 85.06 to 85.12.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification -- panels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in subsections 85.02(2) and (3) and
  sections 85.06 to 85.12 to a complaint resolution officer is
  considered to include a reference to a panel.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420934){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420934.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication --- order or summary of order

- **[[85.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall make public

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an order made by a single complaint
    resolution officer

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of the flight to which the order
      relates,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date of departure of the flight that is
      indicated on the complainant's ticket,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any decision contained in the order in regards
      to the issue of whether any flight delay, flight cancellation or
      denial of boarding was within the carrier's control, was within
      the carrier's control but was required for safety reasons or was
      outside the carrier's control, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether or not the complaint
      resolution officer ordered the carrier to provide compensation or
      a refund as set out in the carrier's tariffs or compensation for
      expenses incurred; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), in the case of an order
    made by a panel, the entire order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may, at the request of a complainant or
  carrier, decide to keep confidential any part of an order, other than
  the information referred to in subparagraphs (1)(a)(i) to (iv).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420947){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420947.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part of annual report

**[[85.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.15 .sectionLabel}** The Agency shall,
as part of its annual report, indicate the number and nature of the
complaints filed under subsection 85.04(1), the names of the carriers
against whom the complaints were made, the number of complaints for
which an order was made under subsection 85.07(1) and the systemic
trends observed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420951){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420951.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees and charges

- **[[85.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall establish fees or
  charges for the purpose of recovering all or a portion of the costs
  that the Agency determines to be related to the process of dealing
  with complaints --- other than complaints disposed of under subsection
  85.04(2) --- under sections 85.05 to 85.12.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrier's liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The carriers that are the subject of complaints ---
  other than complaints disposed of under subsection 85.04(2) --- are
  liable for the payment of the fees or charges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before establishing fees or charges, the Agency shall
  consult with any persons or organizations that the Agency considers to
  be interested in the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall publish the fees and charges on its
  Internet site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to His Majesty

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Fees or charges required to be paid under this
  section constitute a debt to His Majesty in right of Canada and may be
  recovered as such in a court of competent jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spending authority

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may spend the amounts obtained under this
  section in the fiscal year in which they are paid or in the next
  fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The [Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4) does not
  apply to the fees and charges referred to in subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 459](#1410652-1420968){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410652-1420968.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-55080 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} classifying air services;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} classifying aircraft;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing liability insurance coverage
    requirements for air services or aircraft;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing financial requirements for each class
    of air service or aircraft;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the issuance, amendment and cancellation
    of permits for the operation of international charters;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the duration and renewal of licences;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the amendment of licences;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting traffic and tariffs, fares, rates,
    charges and terms and conditions of carriage for international
    service, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} providing for the disallowance or suspension by
      the Agency of any tariff, fare, rate or charge,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} providing for the establishment and substitution
      by the Agency of any tariff, fare, rate or charge disallowed by
      the Agency,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Agency to direct a licensee or
      carrier to take the corrective measures that the Agency considers
      appropriate and to pay compensation for any expense incurred by a
      person adversely affected by the licensee's or carrier's failure
      to apply the fares, rates, charges or terms or conditions of
      carriage that are applicable to the service it offers and that
      were set out in its tariffs, if the Agency receives a written
      complaint and, if the complaint is related to any term or
      condition of carriage concerning any obligation prescribed by
      regulations made under subsection 86.11(1), it is filed by the
      person adversely affected,

    - [(iii.1)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Agency to make applicable, to
      some or to all passengers of the same flight as the complainant,
      all or part of the Agency's decision respecting a complaint
      related to any term or condition of carriage concerning any
      obligation prescribed by regulations made under paragraph
      86.11(1)(b), to the extent that it considers appropriate, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} requiring a licensee or carrier to display the
      terms and conditions of carriage for its international service on
      its Internet site, if the site is used for selling the
      international service of the licensee or carrier;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} requiring licensees to file with the Agency any
    documents and information relating to activities under their
    licences that are necessary for the purposes of enabling the Agency
    to exercise its powers and perform its duties and functions under
    this Part and respecting the manner in which and the times at which
    the documents and information are to be filed;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} requiring licensees to include in contracts or
    arrangements with travel wholesalers, tour operators, charterers or
    other persons associated with the provision of air services to the
    public, or to make those contracts and arrangements subject to,
    terms and conditions specified or referred to in the regulations;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} defining words and expressions for the purposes of
    this Part;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} excluding a person from any of the requirements of
    this Part;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} prescribing any matter or thing that by this Part
    is to be prescribed; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} generally for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion not to provide certain relief

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No regulation shall be made under paragraph (1)(l)
  that has the effect of relieving a person from any provision of this
  Part that requires a person to be a Canadian and to have a Canadian
  aviation document and prescribed liability insurance coverage in
  respect of an air service.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 19, s. 26\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 86
- 2000, c. 15, s. 8
- 2007, c. 19, s. 26
- 2018, c. 10, s. 18
:::
