  or a public passenger service provider, exclude an agreement, or any
  specified portion of an agreement, from the application of paragraph
  (1)(b) on the grounds that harm would likely result to the applicant
  if the agreement, or the specified portion, were to be disclosed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 19, s. 44
:::

### [DIVISION VI.2]{.HLabel2}[Liability and Compensation in Case of Railway Accidents Involving Designated Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-56241 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56242 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[152.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.5 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Division.

[[designated good]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[designated good]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} crude oil; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other good that is designated by the
      regulations. ([marchandise désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Fund for Railway Accidents
    Involving Designated Goods established by subsection
    153.4(1). ([Caisse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[railway accident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[railway accident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any accident or
    incident that is associated with the operation,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on a railway, whether or not that railway is
      within the legislative authority of Parliament, by a railway
      company, of rolling stock that contains a designated good that the
      company carries on a shipper's behalf; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on a railway, by a person other than a railway
      company, of rolling stock that contains a designated good that the
      person carries on behalf of a person who sends or receives
      goods. ([accident ferroviaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[152.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.6 .sectionLabel}** This Division
applies only to a railway company that holds a certificate of fitness
that was issued under paragraph 92(1)(b) in respect of the operation of
a railway for which that certificate was issued.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

#### [Liability]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56258 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit of liability of railway company

- **[[152.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A railway company that operates a
  railway that is involved in a railway accident is liable for the
  losses, damages, costs and expenses described in subsection 153(1) up
  to the amount of the minimum liability insurance coverage that the
  company is required to maintain for the operation of the railway under
  paragraph 93.1(1)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability --- jointly and severally, or
  solidarily

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If more than one railway company is liable under
  subsection (1), the companies are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable, each up to the amount of the minimum liability
  insurance coverage that applies to it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The limit of liability referred to in subsection (1)
  does not apply to the railway company if it is proved that the railway
  accident resulted from any act or omission of that company that was
  committed either with intent to cause the accident or recklessly and
  with the knowledge that the accident would probably result.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No proof of fault or negligence

**[[152.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.8 .sectionLabel}** A railway company's
liability under subsection 152.7(1) does not depend on proof of fault or
negligence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability under another Act

**[[152.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.9 .sectionLabel}** If a railway
company is liable, without proof of fault or negligence, under
subsection 152.7(1) and under any other Act with respect to the same
railway accident, the company is liable under that subsection up to the
greater of the limit of liability for an amount that is referred to in
that subsection and the limit up to which the company is liable under
the other Act. If the other Act does not set out a limit of liability,
the limit set out in that subsection does not apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Losses, damages, costs and expenses

- **[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  losses, damages, costs and expenses referred to in subsection 152.7(1)
  are the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all actual loss or damage incurred by any person,
    other than by a railway company that is liable under subsection
    152.7(1), as a result of the railway accident or as a result of any
    action or measures taken in relation to the accident;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs and expenses reasonably incurred by Her
    Majesty in right of Canada or a province or any other person in
    taking any action or measures in relation to the railway accident;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all loss of non-use value relating to a public
    resource that is affected by the railway accident or as a result of
    any action or measures taken in relation to the accident.

- Definition of [[actual loss or damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), [[actual loss
  or damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes loss of income, including
  future income, and, with respect to any Aboriginal peoples of Canada,
  loss of hunting, fishing and gathering opportunities. It does not
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any loss or damage incurred by a person who
    operates a railway that is not within the legislative authority of
    Parliament and that is involved in the railway accident, in respect
    of the portion of the operation that does not relate to a passenger
    rail service;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any loss of or damage to goods being carried by the
    railway company or by the person referred to in paragraph (a); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any loss of income that is recoverable under
    subsection 42(3) of the [Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Environmental damage

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the measures
  include remedial measures that are taken to repair, reduce or mitigate
  environmental damage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs and expenses not recoverable
  under [Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The costs and expenses that are recoverable by Her
  Majesty in right of Canada or a province under this Division are not
  recoverable under subsection 42(1) of the [Fisheries
  Act](/eng/acts/F-14).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 153
- 2000, c. 16, s. 10
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defences

**[[153.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1 .sectionLabel}** A railway company
is not liable under subsection 152.7(1) if it establishes that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the railway accident resulted from an act of war,
  hostilities, civil war or insurrection; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other defence set out in the regulations applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claims

- **[[153.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All claims for the losses, damages,
  costs and expenses described in subsection 153(1) may be sued for and
  recovered in any court of competent jurisdiction in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rank of claims

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Claims to recover the losses, damages, costs and
  expenses described in paragraphs 153(1)(a) and (b) shall rank, without
  preference, before those to recover a loss of non-use value described
  in paragraph 153(1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation or prescription period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Proceedings in respect of the claims referred to in
  subsection (1) may be instituted within a period of three years
  beginning on the day on which the losses, damages, costs and expenses
  were incurred but not after a period of six years beginning on the day
  on which the railway accident occurred.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings --- loss of non-use value

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Only Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province may
  institute proceedings to recover a loss of non-use value described in
  paragraph 153(1)(c).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Railway company's rights against third
parties

**[[153.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.3 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this
Division shall be construed as limiting or restricting any right of
recourse that a railway company may have against another person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

#### [Fund for Railway Accidents Involving Designated Goods]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56314 .Subheading}

##### [Establishment of Fund]{.HTitleText4} {#h-56315 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fund established

- **[[153.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There is established in the accounts
  of Canada an account to be known as the Fund for Railway Accidents
  Involving Designated Goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following are to be credited to the Fund:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every amount credited to the Fund under section
    153.5;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of every sum credited to the Fund under
    section 153.6;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} every amount recovered by the Administrator under
    paragraph 155(2)(c); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} every amount paid under sections 155.7 and 155.8.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charges

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following are to be charged to the Fund:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amount equal to every amount required to repay,
    in accordance with any terms and conditions specified by the
    Minister of Finance, an amount charged to the Consolidated Revenue
    Fund under section 153.6;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every amount paid out of the Consolidated Revenue
    Fund under subsection 154.2(3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} every amount paid as a result of subsection 155(1);
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the amount of any judgment and any costs awarded as
    the result of an appeal made under subsection 154.9(2) or section
    155.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest to be credited to Fund

**[[153.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.5 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of
Finance shall cause to be credited to the Fund interest, calculated in
the manner and at the rate determined by the Governor in Council, on the
balance of that Fund.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consolidated Revenue Fund

**[[153.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.6 .sectionLabel}** If the amount to
the credit of the Fund is insufficient to pay any amount that is charged
to the Fund under any of paragraphs 153.4(3)(b) to (d), the Minister of
Finance may, subject to any terms and conditions that he or she
considers appropriate, direct that a sum equal to the amount required to
be paid be charged to the Consolidated Revenue Fund and credited to the
Fund.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

##### [Administrator and Deputy Administrator]{.HTitleText4} {#h-56341 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of Administrator

- **[[153.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council shall
  appoint an Administrator of the Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tenure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator is to hold office during good
  behaviour for a term of not more than five years to be fixed by the
  Governor in Council, but may be removed for cause by the Governor in
  Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reappointment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator is eligible for reappointment on
  the expiry of his or her term of office.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation in office

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an Administrator is not appointed to take office
  on the expiry of the incumbent Administrator's term, the incumbent
  continues to hold office until the earlier of the day fixed by the
  Governor in Council and the day on which a successor is appointed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of Deputy Administrator

- **[[153.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may appoint
  a Deputy Administrator of the Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tenure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Deputy Administrator is to hold office during
  good behaviour for a term of not more than five years to be fixed by
  the Governor in Council, but may be removed for cause by the Governor
  in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reappointment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Deputy Administrator is eligible for
  reappointment on the expiry of his or her term of office.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deputy Administrator's powers, duties and
functions

- **[[153.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Deputy Administrator is to
  exercise the powers and perform the duties and functions consistent
  with this Division that are assigned to him or her by the
  Administrator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrator's absence or incapacity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Administrator is absent or incapacitated or
  the office of Administrator is vacant, the Deputy Administrator has
  all the powers, duties and functions of the Administrator.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resignation

**[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154 .sectionLabel}** The resignation of an
Administrator or Deputy Administrator becomes effective at the time that
the Minister receives a written resignation from him or her or at the
time specified in the resignation, whichever is later.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 154
- 2000, c. 16, s. 10
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict of interest

- **[[154.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator or Deputy
  Administrator shall not accept or hold any office or employment, or
  carry on any activity, that is inconsistent with his or her powers,
  duties and functions under this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of contravention

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Administrator or Deputy Administrator
  contravenes subsection (1), his or her appointment is terminated on a
  day fixed by the Governor in Council that is not later than 30 days
  after the day on which the notice of the contravention is received by
  the Minister, but the contravention does not affect the validity of
  any act performed by the Administrator or Deputy Administrator, as the
  case may be, under this Division between the day on which the
  contravention occurred and the day on which the appointment is
  terminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration

- **[[154.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator and the Deputy
  Administrator are to be paid, for the exercise of their powers and the
  performance of their duties and functions, the remuneration fixed by
  the Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Travelling, living and other expenses

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator and the Deputy Administrator are to
  be paid, in accordance with Treasury Board directives, reasonable
  travel, living and other expenses incurred in exercising their powers
  or performing their duties and functions under this Division while
  absent from their ordinary place of work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment out of Consolidated Revenue
  Fund

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the direction of the Minister of Finance, the
  remuneration and expenses referred to in subsections (1) and (2) and
  all other costs and expenses incurred by the Administrator and the
  Deputy Administrator in exercising their powers and performing their
  duties and functions under this Division are to be paid out of the
  Consolidated Revenue Fund and charged to the Fund as provided for by
  paragraph 153.4(3)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Court's assessment officers may, at the
  Minister of Justice's request, tax any account for costs and expenses,
  other than expenses referred to in subsection (2), incurred by the
  Administrator and the Deputy Administrator in exercising their powers
  or performing their duties and functions under this Division as if
  they were acting for Her Majesty in proceedings in that Court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance

**[[154.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.3 .sectionLabel}** The Administrator
may, for the purpose of exercising his or her powers or performing his
or her duties and functions under this Division, obtain any
professional, technical or other advice or assistance that he or she
considers necessary.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

##### [Claims]{.HTitleText4} {#h-56395 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to file claim

- **[[154.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person who incurs a loss, damage,
  cost or expense described in subsection 153(1) as the result of a
  railway accident may file a claim with the Administrator for the
  amount of the loss, damage, cost or expense. The claim must be filed
  within a period of three years beginning on the day on which the loss,
  damage, cost or expense was incurred, but not after a period of six
  years beginning on the day on which the railway accident occurred.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Burden of proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The claimant is not required to satisfy the
  Administrator that the loss, damage, cost or expense resulted from the
  railway accident, but the Administrator shall dismiss the claim if he
  or she is satisfied that the loss, damage, cost or expense did not
  result from the railway accident.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest

- **[[154.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Division,
  interest, in respect of the amount of an offer of compensation for a
  claim made under section 154.4 is deemed to have accrued, at the rate
  referred to in subsection (2), from the day on which the loss, damage,
  cost or expense to which the offer relates was incurred.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The rate for the purposes of subsection (1) is the
  rate prescribed under the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) for
  amounts payable by the Minister of National Revenue as refunds of
  overpayments of tax under that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigation and assessment

- **[[154.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a claim made under
  section 154.4, the Administrator shall investigate and assess it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors to be considered

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In investigating and assessing a claim, the
  Administrator shall consider whether the claim resulted wholly or
  partially from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an act done or omitted to be done by the claimant
    with the intent to incur a loss, damage, cost or expense; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's negligence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When claimant at fault

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall reduce or nullify any amount
  of a claim that he or she would have otherwise assessed in proportion
  to the degree to which he or she is satisfied that the claim resulted
  from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an act done or omitted to be done by the claimant
    with the intent to incur a loss, damage, cost or expense; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's negligence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer of compensation --- one railway
company

- **[[154.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If only one railway company is
  liable under subsection 152.7(1) for the losses, damages, costs or
  expenses described in subsection 153(1) that resulted from the railway
  accident to which a claim made under section 154.4 relates, the
  Administrator shall --- if he or she is satisfied that the railway
  company has paid, in respect of all losses, damages, costs or expenses
  described in subsection 153(1) that resulted from the railway
  accident, an amount equal to or greater than the amount of the minimum
  liability insurance coverage that the railway company was required to
  maintain under paragraph 93.1(1)(b) for the operation of the railway
  involved in the railway accident --- make an offer of compensation to
  the claimant for whatever portion of the claim that the Administrator
  finds to be established and for which the railway company has not
  provided compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer of compensation --- more than one
  railway company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If two or more railway companies are liable under
  subsection 152.7(1) for the losses, damages, costs or expenses
  described in subsection 153(1) that resulted from the railway accident
  to which a claim made under section 154.4 relates, the Administrator
  shall --- if he or she is satisfied that the railway companies have
  paid, in respect of all losses, damages, costs or expenses described
  in subsection 153(1) that resulted from the railway accident, an
  amount equal to or greater than the sum that is equal to the total of
  the amount of the minimum liability insurance coverage that each of
  the railway companies was required to maintain under paragraph
  93.1(1)(b) for the operation of its railway involved in the railway
  accident --- make an offer of compensation to the claimant for
  whatever portion of the claim that the Administrator finds to be
  established and for which the railway companies have not provided
  compensation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrator's powers

**[[154.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.8 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
investigating and assessing a claim and determining the amount, if any,
that a railway company has paid as compensation in relation to the
railway accident to which the claim relates, the Administrator has the
powers of a commissioner under Part I of the [Inquiries
Act](/eng/acts/I-11).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed refusal

- **[[154.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Administrator makes an offer
  of compensation to a claimant and the claimant does not, within 60
  days after receiving the offer, or any longer period that the
  Administrator allows, notify the Administrator whether they accept or
  reject it, the claimant is deemed to have refused the offer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal to Federal Court --- adequacy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The claimant may, before the expiry of the applicable
  period under subsection (1), appeal the adequacy of the offer to the
  Federal Court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acceptance of offer

- **[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  claimant accepts an offer of compensation from the Administrator, the
  Administrator shall without delay direct that payment be made to the
  claimant of the amount of the offer of compensation and an amount
  equal to the interest that is deemed to have accrued under subsection
  154.5(1) in respect of that amount.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of acceptance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant accepts an offer of compensation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant is precluded from pursuing any rights
    that they may have against any person in respect of the losses,
    damages, costs or expenses to which the offer of compensation
    relates;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Administrator is, to the extent of the payment
    to the claimant, subrogated to any rights of the claimant in respect
    of those losses, damages, costs or expenses; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Administrator shall take reasonable measures to
    recover the amount of the payment from

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the railway company --- or from any railway
      company --- that is liable under subsection 152.7(1) for those
      losses, damages, costs or expenses, if the limit of liability
      referred to in subsection 152.7(1) does not apply to the railway
      company by reason of subsection 152.7(3) or section 152.9, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other person who is liable for those losses,
      damages, costs or expenses.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commencement of action

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator may, for the purpose of paragraph
  (2)(c), commence an action in the Administrator's or the claimant's
  name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a railway company's limit of liability for the
  railway accident to which the offer of compensation relates is, by
  reason of section 152.9, higher than its limit of liability under
  subsection 152.7(1), nothing in paragraph (2)(c) is to be construed as
  permitting the Administrator to recover from the railway company an
  amount that is greater than the difference between the two limits of
  liability.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 155
- 2000, c. 16, s. 10
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal to Federal Court --- notice of
dismissal

**[[155.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.1 .sectionLabel}** A claimant who has
received a notification that the Administrator has dismissed their claim
may, within 60 days after receiving the notification, appeal the
dismissal to the Federal Court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

##### [Proceedings Against Railway Company]{.HTitleText4} {#h-56461 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings against railway company

- **[[155.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If proceedings are commenced against
  a railway company in respect of any loss, damage, cost or expense
  described in subsection 153(1) that resulted from a railway accident,
  the railway company shall as soon as feasible provide the
  Administrator with a copy of the document commencing the proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrator is party

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator is a party to the proceedings
  referred to in subsection (1) and may appear for the purpose of taking
  any measure that he or she considers appropriate for the proper
  administration of the Fund.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

##### [Levies]{.HTitleText4} {#h-56469 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Levy --- crude oil

**[[155.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.3 .sectionLabel}** The levy for the
carriage, on a railway, of crude oil is \$1.65 per tonne in the year
ending on March 31, 2016, adjusted annually in accordance with section
155.4.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual adjustment of levy

- **[[155.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The levy for the carriage, on a
  railway, of crude oil is to be adjusted annually so that the levy in
  any following year is an amount equal to the product of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the levy that would have been payable in that
    following year if no adjustment had been made under this section
    with respect to that following year, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ratio that the Consumer Price Index for the
    calendar year next before that following year bears to the Consumer
    Price Index for the calendar year next before that calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer Price Index

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference to the Consumer Price Index for any
    12-month period means the average of the Consumer Price Index for
    Canada, as published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for each month in that 12-month
    period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if at any time the Consumer Price Index for Canada,
    as published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), is adjusted to reflect a new time
    basis, a corresponding adjustment is to be made in the Consumer
    Price Index for any 12-month period that is used for the purpose of
    calculating the levy under this section; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if at any time the Consumer Price Index for Canada,
    as published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), is adjusted to reflect a new
    content basis, that adjustment does not affect the operation of this
    section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, make regulations respecting the manner in which the
  average of the Consumer Price Index for any 12-month period is to be
  determined and the manner of expressing any such average that is
  determined to be a fraction of a whole number.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjusted levy to be published

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause the levy for the carriage of
  crude oil that is adjusted in accordance with this section to be
  published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) as soon
  as it is available, and the levy so published is admissible in any
  proceedings as conclusive proof of the levy for the year in question.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Levy --- other traffic

**[[155.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.5 .sectionLabel}** The levy for the
carriage, on a railway, of any traffic, other than crude oil, that is
specified in the regulations --- or for the carriage of any traffic or
any class of traffic, other than crude oil, that is specified in the
regulations --- is the amount specified in the regulations, or
calculated in accordance with them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional levy

- **[[155.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an amount is charged to the
  Consolidated Revenue Fund under section 153.6, the Minister may, by
  order, direct that every railway company that, on a railway, carries
  traffic for which there is a levy under section 155.3 or 155.5 pay, in
  accordance with the order, an additional levy in the amount specified
  in the order, or calculated in accordance with it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If such an order is made, the Minister shall revoke
  it as soon as feasible after an amount equal to the amount charged to
  the Consolidated Revenue Fund under section 153.6 has been credited to
  the Consolidated Revenue Fund out of amounts standing to the credit of
  the Fund and the Minister is satisfied that the terms and conditions
  in relation to the charging of that amount under that section have
  been met.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to pay

- **[[155.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every railway company that is the
  first to carry, at a rate other than an interswitching rate, traffic
  in respect of which there is a levy under section 155.3 or 155.5 after
  the traffic has been loaded --- and every railway company that is
  directed by an order made under section 155.6 to pay a levy --- shall
  pay to the Receiver General an amount equal to the amount of the levy,
  applicable to the traffic carried by it, or that is required to be
  paid under the order, as the case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 30 days after the expiry of the calendar
    quarter in which the levy became due; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a regulation made under paragraph 155.97(d) is
    in force, before the expiry of the period specified in or determined
    under that regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When levy is due --- sections 155.3 and
  155.5

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A levy under section 155.3 or 155.5 becomes due on
  the first day on which the traffic is carried, at a rate other than an
  interswitching rate, by the railway company that is the first to carry
  the traffic after it has been loaded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When levy is due --- section 155.6

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A levy imposed by an order made under section 155.6
  becomes due on the day provided for in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[calendar
  quarter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[calendar
  quarter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a three-month period that begins
  on the first day of January, April, July or October.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
- 2018, c. 10, s. 59(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155.7-20160618.html "Link to previous version of section 155.7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on unpaid amounts

- **[[155.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If any portion of a levy is not paid
  by a railway company as required by subsection 155.7(1), the company
  shall pay to the Receiver General interest on that portion ---
  calculated and compounded monthly at the rate of interest determined
  under the regulations made under section 155.1 of the [Financial
  Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) --- beginning on the day on which
  the payment was required to be made and ending on the day before the
  day on which the payment is received by the Receiver General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partial payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a partial payment is made, the period for which
  interest is payable in respect of the amount paid ends on the day
  before the day on which the partial payment is received by the
  Receiver General.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
- 2018, c. 10, s. 43
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155.8-20160618.html "Link to previous version of section 155.8"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Her Majesty

**[[155.81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.81 .sectionLabel}** All amounts
payable under subsection 155.7(1) and section 155.8 are debts due to Her
Majesty in right of Canada and are recoverable in any court of competent
jurisdiction from the railway company that is required to pay them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to require information

**[[155.82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.82 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
direct that a railway company provide, in the specified form and within
the specified period, information or documents that he or she considers
necessary for the purposes of ensuring compliance with this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discontinuation and reimposition

- **[[155.83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.83
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order,
  discontinue the application of subsections 113(2.1) and 155.7(1) in
  respect of the carriage of any traffic --- indefinitely or until a
  time specified in the order --- or, by order, reimpose the application
  of those subsections in respect of the carriage of that traffic if it
  has been discontinued indefinitely.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual adjustment of levy unaffected

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The making of an order under subsection (1) does not
  affect the operation of section 155.4 in respect of any levy.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records and books of account

- **[[155.84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.84
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every railway company that is
  required to pay an amount under subsection 155.7(1) shall keep at
  their place of business in Canada, or at any other place in Canada
  that may be designated by the Minister, records and books of account
  that set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount that is payable by the railway company
    under that subsection;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the type and quantity of the traffic in respect of
    which that amount is payable;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the traffic to which that amount relates is
    traffic in respect of which there is a levy under section 155.3 or
    155.5, the day on which the traffic was first carried, at a rate
    other than an interswitching rate, by the railway company after it
    was loaded and the place from which it was first carried by it after
    the loading; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the traffic to which that amount relates is
    traffic in respect of which there is a levy imposed by an order made
    under section 155.6, the day on which the traffic was carried by the
    railway company, the place from which it was carried and the place
    to which it was carried.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposal of records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every railway company that is required by
  subsection (1) to keep records and books of account shall, unless
  otherwise authorized by the Minister, retain those records and books
  of account, and every account or voucher that is necessary to verify
  the information contained in them, until the expiry of six years after
  the end of the year to which the records or books of account relate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination of records

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every railway company that is required by
  subsection (1) to keep records and books of account shall, at all
  reasonable times, make the records and books of account, and every
  account or voucher that is necessary to verify the information
  contained in them, available to any person designated under subsection
  155.85(1) and give that person every facility that is necessary to
  examine them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
- 2018, c. 10, s. 59(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155.84-20160618.html "Link to previous version of section 155.84"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

- **[[155.85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.85
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may designate persons
  or classes of persons for the purposes of the administration and
  enforcement of subsection 155.7(1) or section 155.84.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is designated under subsection (1)
  is to be provided with a certificate of their designation that must be
  produced, on request, to the owner, occupier or person in charge of
  any place referred to in subsection 155.86(1) when the designated
  person enters the place.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection

- **[[155.86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.86
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person who is designated under
  subsection 155.85(1) may, for a purpose related to verifying
  compliance with subsection 155.7(1) or section 155.84, enter any place
  in which they have reasonable grounds to believe there are any
  records, books of account, accounts, vouchers or other documents
  relating to amounts payable under subsection 155.7(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on entry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The designated person may, for the purpose referred
  to in subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} examine anything in the place, including any
    document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use any means of communication in the place, or
    cause it to be used;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} use any computer system in the place, or cause it
    to be used, to examine data contained in or available to it, or
    reproduce the data, or cause it to be reproduced, in the form of a
    printout or other intelligible output and remove any printout or
    output for examination or copying;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prepare a document, or cause one to be prepared,
    based on the data;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} use any copying equipment in the place, or cause it
    to be used;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} take photographs or make recordings or sketches of
    anything in the place;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prohibit or limit access to all or part of the
    place or to anything in the place; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} remove anything from the place for the purpose of
    examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons accompanying designated person

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The designated person may be accompanied by any
  person who they believe is necessary to help them exercise their
  powers or perform their duties or functions under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The owner or person in charge of the place and every
  person in the place shall give all assistance that is reasonably
  required to enable the designated person to exercise their powers or
  perform their duties or functions under this section and shall provide
  any documents or information, and access to any data, that is
  reasonably required.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant to enter dwelling-house

- **[[155.87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.87
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the place referred to in
  subsection 155.86(1) is a dwelling-house, the designated person may
  enter it without the occupant's consent only under the authority of a
  warrant issued under subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to issue warrant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application, a
  justice of the peace may issue a warrant authorizing the designated
  person to enter a dwelling-house, subject to any conditions specified
  in the warrant, if the justice is satisfied by information on oath
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the dwelling-house is a place referred to in
    subsection 155.86(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entry to the dwelling-house is necessary for the
    purpose of that subsection; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entry was refused by the occupant or there are
    reasonable grounds to believe that entry will be refused by, or that
    consent to entry cannot be obtained from, the occupant.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to Minister

**[[155.88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.88 .sectionLabel}** On the conclusion
of an inspection, the designated person shall provide a written report
of their findings to the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of documents

- **[[155.89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.89
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A document that is removed under
  paragraph 155.86(2)(h) must be returned to the person from whose
  custody it is taken within 21 days after it is taken or within any
  longer period that is directed by a judge of a superior court or
  agreed to by a person who is entitled to its return.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of application for extension of
  time

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application to a judge for a direction under
  subsection (1) may only be made on notice to the person from whose
  custody the document is removed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of documents

**[[155.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.9 .sectionLabel}** A document
purporting to be certified by the Minister to be a copy of a document
made under subsection 155.86(2) is admissible in evidence in any
prosecution for an offence in respect of a contravention of a provision
of this Division or of a regulation made under this Division --- or in
respect of a contravention of an order made under this Division --- and,
in the absence of evidence to the contrary, is proof of its contents.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False information, etc.

- **[[155.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall knowingly make any
  false or misleading statement, or knowingly provide false or
  misleading information, either orally or in writing, to a person who
  is exercising powers or performing duties or functions under section
  155.86.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obstruction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall knowingly obstruct or hinder a person
  who is exercising powers or performing duties or functions under
  section 155.86.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

##### [Administration of Fund]{.HTitleText4} {#h-56607 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Books of account and systems

- **[[155.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall cause

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} records and books of account to be kept in relation
    to the Fund; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} control and information systems and management
    practices, in respect of financial and management matters, to be
    maintained in relation to the Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrator's responsibility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall keep or maintain, as the case
  may be, the records, books, systems and practices in a manner that
  provides reasonable assurance that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Administrator's and Deputy Administrator's
    powers, duties and functions under this Division are exercised and
    performed effectively and in accordance with this Division;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the assets used by them are safeguarded and
    controlled; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the financial, human and physical resources used by
    them are managed economically and efficiently.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual report

- **[[155.93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.93
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall as soon as
  feasible, but in any case within three months after the end of each
  fiscal year, submit an annual report, in any form that the Minister
  may direct, on the Administrator's activities in that year to the
  Minister, who shall cause a copy of the report to be laid before each
  House of Parliament on any of the first 15 days on which that House is
  sitting after he or she receives it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The annual report must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the amounts charged and credited to
    the Fund during the fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the costs and expenses incurred
    during the fiscal year by the Administrator and the Deputy
    Administrator in exercising their powers and performing their duties
    and functions under this Division;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the fees for services rendered by
    the Administrator and the Deputy Administrator during the fiscal
    year;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the auditor's report with respect to the statements
    referred to in paragraphs (a) to (c); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the costs of preparing the auditor's report.

- Definition of [[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means the period that begins on April 1 in one calendar year and that
  ends on March 31 in the next calendar year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special examination

- **[[155.94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.94
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall cause a
  special examination to be carried out in respect of the systems and
  practices referred to in paragraph 155.92(1)(b) to determine if they
  were, in the period under examination, maintained in a manner that
  provided reasonable assurance that the assets referred to in paragraph
  155.92(2)(b) were safeguarded and controlled and that the resources
  referred to in paragraph 155.92(2)(c) were managed economically and
  efficiently.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for examination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A special examination must be carried out at least
  once every five years by the Administrator and at any other time that
  the Governor in Council or Minister may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examiner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall appoint a person to act as
  examiner for the purpose of conducting a special examination. However,
  if a special examination is required by the Governor in Council or the
  Minister, the Governor in Council or the Minister, as the case may be,
  shall make the appointment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict of interest

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The examiner shall not accept or hold any office or
  employment, or carry on any activity, that is inconsistent with their
  duties and functions under this section and section 155.95.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Before an examiner begins a special examination, they
  shall survey the systems and practices to be examined and submit to
  the Minister and the Administrator a plan for the examination,
  including a statement of the criteria to be applied in the
  examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution of disagreements

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Any disagreement between the examiner and the
  Administrator with respect to the plan is to be resolved by the
  Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

- **[[155.95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.95
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the conclusion of a special
  examination, the examiner shall provide a written report of their
  findings to the Minister and the Administrator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The examiner's report must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether, in their opinion, with
    respect to the criteria established under subsection 155.94(5),
    there is reasonable assurance that there are no significant
    deficiencies in the systems and practices examined; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the extent to which they relied on
    internal audits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to information

- **[[155.96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.96
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the examiner considers it
  necessary to enable them to prepare a report as required by this
  Division, they may direct that the present or any former Administrator
  or Deputy Administrator --- or any present or former employee or agent
  or mandatary of the present or any former Administrator or Deputy
  Administrator --- provide, to the extent that that person is
  reasonably able to do so, the examiner with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information and explanations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} access to any records, books of account, accounts,
    vouchers and other documents related to the Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrator's responsibilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the examiner's request, the Administrator shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide any information and explanations that the
    examiner considers necessary to enable the examiner to prepare any
    report that is required by this Division and that the Administrator
    is reasonably able to provide; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} obtain from any former Administrator or the present
    or any former Deputy Administrator --- or from any present or any
    former employee or agent or mandatary of the present or former
    Administrator or Deputy Administrator --- any information and
    explanations that the examiner considers necessary to enable the
    examiner to prepare any report that is required by this Division and
    that any of those persons are reasonably able to provide, and then
    provide the examiner with the information and explanations so
    obtained.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

#### [Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56669 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Governor in Council

**[[155.97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.97 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating goods or classes of goods for the purpose
  of the definition [[designated good]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
  152.5;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} setting out defences for the purpose of section
  153.1;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting traffic or any class of traffic for the
  purposes of section 155.5 and a levy for the carriage of that traffic
  or class of traffic, or a manner of calculating the levy;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting a period for the purpose of subsection
  155.7(1);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the keeping, by railway companies, of
  information in relation to their carriage of any traffic in respect of
  which there is a levy under section 155.3 or 155.5, or a levy imposed
  by an order made under section 155.6, and the filing of that
  information with the Minister;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 10, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} generally for carrying out the purposes and
  provisions of this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
- 2018, c. 10, s. 44
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155.97-20160618.html "Link to previous version of section 155.97"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56681 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sections 26 and 37

**[[155.98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.98 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of sections 26 and 37, this Division is deemed not to be a part of an
Act of Parliament that is referred to in those sections.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 31, s. 10
:::

### [DIVISION VII]{.HLabel2}[Other Matters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-56686 .Subheading}

#### [Accounting]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56687 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uniform accounting system for CN and CP

- **[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency may prescribe for the Canadian National Railway Company and the
  Canadian Pacific Limited a uniform classification and system of
  accounts of their assets, liabilities, revenues, working expenditures,
  capitalization, traffic and operating statistics relating to railway
  operations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uniform accounting system for other
  railway companies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may prescribe for any other railway
  company a uniform classification and system as described in
  subsection (1) or in a condensed form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Items to be classed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may prescribe the items to be classed as
  items relating to railway operations in the accounts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Depreciation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may prescribe the classes of property for
  which depreciation charges may properly be included under operating
  expenses in the accounts, and the rates of depreciation to be charged
  with respect to each of the classes of property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements to keep accounts

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A railway company for which a classification and
  system of accounts is prescribed shall keep its accounts in accordance
  with the prescribed classification and system.

#### [Determination of Costs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56699 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations for determining costs

- **[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency may make regulations prescribing items and factors that it
  shall consider in determining costs under this Part, including
  depreciation and the cost of capital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional considerations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may also consider

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the principles of costing adopted by the Royal
    Commission on Transportation appointed by the Order in Council dated
    May 13, 1959 in arriving at the conclusions contained in its report;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} later developments in railway costing methods and
    techniques and current conditions of railway operations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Computation of costs of a portion of an
  undertaking

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the costs of a portion of the railway of a railway
  company, or one of its operations, are to be computed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a particular period, the Agency must include in
    the computation any of the costs of the whole railway, or any other
    portion of it, that, in the opinion of the Agency, are reasonably
    attributable to the portion or operation, irrespective of when, in
    what manner or by whom the costs were incurred; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of future operations of the company, the
    costs must be determined in accordance with estimates made on any
    basis that, in the opinion of the Agency, is reasonable in the
    circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination final and binding

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A determination of costs by the Agency under this
  Part is final and binding on all interested or affected parties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costing information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No later than August 31 of every year, the Canadian
  National Railway Company and the Canadian Pacific Railway Company
  shall provide to the Agency, in the form and manner specified by the
  Agency, all unit costs, output units and other financial, statistical
  and supporting information for the preceding calendar year that is
  required for the determination of costs by the Agency under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 157
- 2018, c. 10, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-157-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 157"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Agreements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-56717 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements to apply transportation law to
provincial railways

- **[[157.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may enter into an
  agreement with a provincial minister responsible for transportation
  matters providing for the administration, in relation to persons who
  operate railways within the legislative authority of the province, of
  any law respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} accident investigations and railway crossings; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} railway noise and vibration, or the regulation of
    the rates and conditions of service of railway companies, to the
    extent that those matters are governed by this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may designate any body established under
  an Act of Parliament, or any person or class of persons employed in
  the federal public administration, to administer the law in accordance
  with the agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Functions, duties and powers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The designated body, person or class of persons may
  perform any function or duty and exercise any powers necessary for the
  enforcement of the law, to the extent specified in the agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 157.1
- 2003, c. 22, s. 224(E)
- 2007, c. 19, s. 46
- 2012, c. 7, s. 39
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-157.1-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 157.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements made with provincial
authorities

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may enter
into an agreement with a provincial authority to authorize the
provincial authority to regulate the construction and operation of a
railway as well as the rates and conditions of service in the same
manner and to the same extent as it may regulate a railway within its
jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 158
- 2007, c. 19, s. 47
- 2012, c. 7, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 158"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Arbitrations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-56733 .Part}

### [DIVISION I]{.HLabel2}[Final Offer Arbitration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-56734 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of sections 161 to 169

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 161 to 169 apply only in
  respect of matters arising between shippers and carriers that involve

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carriage of goods by air to which Part II
    applies, other than their carriage internationally;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the carriage of goods by railways to which this Act
    applies, other than the carriage of goods in trailers or containers
    on flat cars unless the containers arrive by water at a port in
    Canada, served by only one railway company, for further movement by
    rail or arrive by rail at such a port in Canada for further movement
    by water; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the carriage by water, for hire or reward, of goods
    required for the maintenance or development of a municipality or any
    permanent settlement for northern marine resupply purposes, other
    than goods required in relation to national defence or in relation
    to the exploration for or the development, extraction or processing
    of oil, gas or any mineral.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scope of paragraph (1)(c)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(c) applies only to resupply services on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rivers, streams, lakes and other waters within
    the watershed of the Mackenzie River;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the territorial sea and internal waters of Canada
    that are adjacent to the coast of the mainland and islands of the
    Canadian Arctic and situated within the area bounded by the
    meridians of longitude 95° West and 141° West and the parallels of
    latitude 66° 00′30″ North and 74°00′20″ North; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the internal waters of Canada comprised in Spence
    Bay and Shepherd Bay and situated east of the meridian of longitude
    95° West.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(c) applies only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total register tonnage of all ships used to
    provide the resupply service exceeds fifty register tons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the resupply service originates from a point
    situated on the waters described in subsection (2).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rail passenger services

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** Sections 161 to 169
also apply, with any modifications that the circumstances require, in
respect of the rates charged or proposed to be charged by, and in
respect of any of the conditions associated with the provision of
services by, a railway company to any other railway company engaged in
passenger rail services, except a public passenger service provider as
defined in section 87.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 160
- 2008, c. 5, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 160"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission for final offer arbitration

- **[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  shipper who is dissatisfied with the rate or rates charged or proposed
  to be charged by a carrier for the movement of goods, or with any of
  the conditions associated with the movement of goods, may, if the
  matter cannot be resolved between the shipper and the carrier, submit
  the matter in writing to the Agency for a final offer arbitration to
  be conducted by one arbitrator or, if the shipper and the carrier
  agree, by a panel of three arbitrators.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of submission

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A copy of a submission under subsection (1) shall be
  served on the carrier by the shipper and the submission shall contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the final offer of the shipper to the carrier in
    the matter, excluding any dollar amounts;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period requested by the shipper, not exceeding
    two years, for which the decision of the arbitrator is to apply;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking by the shipper to ship the goods to
    which the arbitration relates in accordance with the decision of the
    arbitrator;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking by the shipper to the Agency whereby
    the shipper agrees to pay to the arbitrator the fee for which the
    shipper is liable under section 166 as a party to the arbitration;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name of the arbitrator, if any, that the
    shipper and the carrier agreed should conduct the arbitration or, if
    they agreed that the arbitration should be conducted by a panel of
    three arbitrators, the name of an arbitrator chosen by the shipper
    and the name of an arbitrator chosen by the carrier.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arbitration precluded in certain cases

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall not have any matter submitted to it
  by a shipper under subsection (1) arbitrated if the shipper has not,
  at least five days before making the submission, served on the carrier
  a written notice indicating that the shipper intends to submit the
  matter to the Agency for a final offer arbitration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final offer arbitration not a
  proceeding

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A final offer arbitration is not a proceeding before
  the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 161
- 2000, c. 16, s. 11
- 2018, c. 10, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-161-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 161"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of final offers

- **[[161.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within 10 days after a submission is
  served under subsection 161(2), the shipper and the carrier shall
  submit to the Agency their final offers, including dollar amounts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies to the parties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without delay after final offers are submitted under
  subsection (1) by both the shipper and the carrier, the Agency shall
  provide the shipper and the carrier with copies of each other's final
  offer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If no final offer from a party

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If one party does not submit a final offer in
  accordance with subsection (1), the final offer submitted by the other
  party is deemed to be the final offer selected by the arbitrator under
  subsection 165(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 16, s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arbitration

- **[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any application
  filed with the Agency by a carrier in respect of a matter, within five
  days after final offers are received under subsection 161.1(1), the
  Agency shall refer the matter for arbitration

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the parties did not agree that the arbitration
    should be conducted by a panel of three arbitrators, to the
    arbitrator, if any, named under paragraph 161(2)(e) or, if that
    arbitrator is not, in the opinion of the Agency, available to
    conduct the arbitration or no arbitrator is named, to an arbitrator
    on the list of arbitrators referred to in section 169 who the Agency
    chooses and determines is appropriate and available to conduct the
    arbitration; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the parties agreed that the arbitration should
    be conducted by a panel of three arbitrators,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to the arbitrators named by the parties under
      paragraph 161(2)(e) and to any arbitrator who those arbitrators
      have, within 10 days after the submission was served under
      subsection 161(2), notified the Agency that they have agreed on,
      or if those arbitrators did not so notify the Agency, to an
      arbitrator on the list of arbitrators referred to in section 169
      who the Agency chooses and determines is appropriate and available
      to conduct the arbitration, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if an arbitrator referred to in subparagraph (i)
      is not, in the opinion of the Agency, available to conduct the
      arbitration, to the arbitrators named in that subparagraph who are
      available and to an arbitrator chosen by the Agency from the list
      of arbitrators referred to in section 169 who the Agency
      determines is appropriate and available to conduct the
      arbitration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If a matter was referred to a panel of arbitrators,
  every reference in subsections (1.2) and (2) and sections 163 to 169
  to an arbitrator or the arbitrator shall be construed as a reference
  to a panel of arbitrators or the panel of arbitrators, as the case may
  be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delay in referral

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} If the shipper consents to an application referred
  to in subsection (1) being heard before the matter is referred to an
  arbitrator, the Agency shall defer referring the matter until the
  application is dealt with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance by Agency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Agency may, at the request of the arbitrator,
  provide administrative, technical and legal assistance to the
  arbitrator on a cost recovery basis.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 162
- 2000, c. 16, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision or order affecting a matter
being arbitrated

**[[162.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162.1 .sectionLabel}** The Agency may, in
addition to any other decision or order it may make, order that an
arbitration be discontinued, that it be continued subject to the terms
and conditions that the Agency may fix or that the decision of the
arbitrator be set aside if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Agency makes a decision or an order arising out
  of an application that is in respect of a matter submitted to the
  Agency for a final offer arbitration and that is filed by a carrier
  before the matter is referred to arbitration; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the decision or order affects the arbitration.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 16, s. 14
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

- **[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  the absence of an agreement by the arbitrator and the parties as to
  the procedure to be followed, a final offer arbitration shall be
  governed by the rules of procedure made by the Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure generally

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The arbitrator shall conduct the arbitration
  proceedings as expeditiously as possible and, subject to the procedure
  referred to in subsection (1), in the manner the arbitrator considers
  appropriate having regard to the circumstances of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exchange of information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Within fifteen days after the Agency refers a matter
  for arbitration, the parties shall exchange the information that they
  intend to submit to the arbitrator in support of their final offers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interrogatories

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Within seven days after receipt of the information
  referred to in subsection (3), each party may direct interrogatories
  to the other, which shall be answered within fifteen days after their
  receipt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withholding of information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a party unreasonably withholds information that
  the arbitrator subsequently deems to be relevant, that withholding
  shall be taken into account by the arbitrator in making a decision.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arbitration information

- **[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  arbitrator shall, in conducting a final offer arbitration between a
  shipper and a carrier, have regard to the information provided to the
  arbitrator by the parties in support of their final offers and, unless
  the parties agree to limit the amount of information to be provided,
  to any additional information that is provided by the parties at the
  arbitrator's request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arbitration considerations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the parties agree otherwise, in rendering a
  decision the arbitrator shall have regard to whether there is
  available to the shipper an alternative, effective, adequate and
  competitive means of transporting the goods to which the matter
  relates and to all considerations that appear to the arbitrator to be
  relevant to the matter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary process

**[[164.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164.1 .sectionLabel}** If the Agency
determines that a shipper's final offer submitted under subsection
161.1(1) involves freight charges in an amount of not more than
\$2,000,000, adjusted in accordance with section 164.2, and the shipper
did not indicate a contrary intention when submitting the offer,
sections 163 and 164 do not apply and the arbitration shall proceed as
follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} within seven days after a matter is referred to an
  arbitrator, the shipper and the carrier may file with the arbitrator a
  response to the final offer of the other party;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (c), the arbitrator shall decide
  the matter on the basis of the final offers and any response filed
  under paragraph (a); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the arbitrator considers it necessary, the
  arbitrator may invite the parties to make oral representations or may
  ask the parties to appear before him or her to provide further
  information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 16, s. 15
- 2018, c. 10, s. 47
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-164.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 164.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Triennial adjustment

- **[[164.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of freight
  charges for the purpose of section 164.1 shall be adjusted every three
  years, on April 1, in accordance with the formula

  ::: {#56826 .Subsection}
  \[A/B\] × C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the Consumer Price Index for the calendar year before the year
      in which the adjustment is made;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the Consumer Price Index for 2017; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is \$2,000,000.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer Price Index

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference to the Consumer Price Index for any
    12-month period means the average of the Consumer Price Index for
    Canada, as published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for each month in that 12-month
    period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if at any time the Consumer Price Index for Canada
    is adjusted to reflect a new time basis, a corresponding adjustment
    is to be made in the Consumer Price Index for any 12-month period
    that is used for the purpose of calculating the maximum amount under
    subsection (1); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if at any time the Consumer Price Index for Canada
    is adjusted to reflect a new content basis, that adjustment does not
    affect the operation of this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount to be published

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Agency shall adjust the maximum amount in
  accordance with subsection (1) and cause it to be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) no later than March 31
  before the commencement of the three-year period for which the maximum
  amount applies, and that published amount is conclusive proof of the
  maximum amount for that three-year period.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 10, s. 48
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision of arbitrator

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  decision of the arbitrator in conducting a final offer arbitration
  shall be the selection by the arbitrator of the final offer of either
  the shipper or the carrier.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements re decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The decision of the arbitrator shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in writing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless the parties agree otherwise, be rendered
    within 60 days or, in the case of an arbitration conducted in
    accordance with section 164.1, 30 days after the date on which the
    submission for the final offer arbitration was received by the
    Agency; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be rendered so as to apply for the period that is
    agreed to by the parties or, if no period has been agreed to by the
    parties, for the period, not exceeding two years, that the shipper
    requested in its submission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation in tariff

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The carrier shall, without delay after the
  arbitrator's decision, set out the rate or rates or the conditions
  associated with the movement of goods that have been selected by the
  arbitrator in a tariff of the carrier, unless, where the carrier is
  entitled to keep the rate or rates or conditions confidential, the
  parties to the arbitration agree to include the rate or rates or
  conditions in a contract that the parties agree to keep confidential.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasons not required

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No reasons shall be set out in the decision of the
  arbitrator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasons may be requested

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The arbitrator shall, if requested by all of the
  parties to the arbitration within 30 days or, in the case of an
  arbitration conducted in accordance with section 164.1, seven days
  after the decision of the arbitrator, give written reasons for the
  decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of decision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Except where both parties agree otherwise,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the decision of the arbitrator on a final offer
    arbitration shall be final and binding and be applicable to the
    parties as of the date on which the submission for the arbitration
    was received by the Agency from the shipper, and is enforceable as
    if it were an order of the Agency; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the arbitrator shall direct in the decision that
    interest at a reasonable rate specified by the arbitrator shall be
    paid to one of the parties by the other on moneys that, as a result
    of the application of paragraph (a), are owed by a party for the
    period between the date referred to in that paragraph and the date
    of the payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment by party

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Moneys and interest referred to in paragraph (6)(b)
  that are owed by a party pursuant to a decision of the arbitrator
  shall be paid without delay to the other party.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 10, s. 165
- 2000, c. 16, s. 16
- 2018, c. 10, s. 49
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-165-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 165"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arbitration fees

- **[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Agency may fix the fee to be paid to an arbitrator for the costs of,
  and the services provided by, the arbitrator in final offer
  arbitration proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees and costs
